# Feature specs — index

One numbered spec per feature unit. The workflow (see `CLAUDE.md`) is: read the
spec for the unit you're told to build **in full**, implement exactly it. This
index is for finding the right spec and tracing related work — not a substitute
for reading the spec itself.

## Writing a spec — required sections

Every new spec carries, per buildable unit, alongside the feature description:

1. **Negative cases.** The failure modes a user can actually hit: RPC error
   codes (unique violations, CHECK rejections, RLS denials), stale-data races
   (row changed/superseded since read), offline/retry on capture surfaces, and
   the empty/zero states of every list or count the unit renders.
2. **Error messages.** The exact user-facing Thai string for each failure mode.
   A string used on 2+ surfaces goes to `src/lib/i18n/labels.ts` (the UI-term
   SSOT); never let a raw Postgres error reach the user.
3. **Recovery path.** What the user does next for each mode: retry, edit and
   resubmit, a refresh hint, or "contact X" — and whether the system self-heals.

Why binding: scope discipline (CLAUDE.md) rejects error handlers the spec did
not ask for — the spec is the only door error handling can legally enter
through. A spec that omits this section ships a unit whose failure modes are
decided by accident at review time. Each listed mode becomes a RED-first test
under the ship-unit RED gate — `throws_ok` for DB-layer modes, RTL for
component modes, an integration/E2E assert where neither reaches (offline,
retry). Specs written before 2026-07-19 are exempt until next touched.

Operator dials: standalone catalog-keyed receipt (no PR/PO touch), per-project store (no stores table), moving-avg cost tracked now. **U2: /store surface** — project picker → on-hand (qty + value + derived moving-avg cost) + `รับเข้าสต๊อก` form (`StoreManager` + `recordStockIn`; settings drill-down); mig 20260809000100 gives the two nullable RPC args `default null`. **U3: เบิก/Issue foundation** — `stock_issues` (append-only, item drawn TO a WP at moving-avg cost) + `issue_stock` RPC (SITE_STAFF gate, decrement on-hand at avg cost, insufficient-stock guard, depletion→value 0); mig 20260809000200, pgTAP 182. **U4: เบิก UI on /store** — per-row เบิก button → WP+qty sheet → `issueStock` (manager-tier gated) + recent-issues list (`StoreManager`, code-only). **U5: เบิก at the WP detail** — `WpIssueStock` block in the คำขอซื้อ tab (site_admin field-draw, gated `!readOnly`); reuses `issueStock`, draws the project on-hand TO this WP. เบิก now has both surfaces (/store + WP detail). **U6: two-party custody handshake** — `stock_issues` += receiver_worker_id + received_at; `issue_stock` names a receiver worker; `confirm_stock_issue` = the receiver attests via the portal (mig 20260809000300, pgTAP 183). **U7: custody UI (issue side)** — receiver picker on the WP-detail เบิก (`WpIssueStock` + project workers) + pending/received badge on both recent-issues lists; `issueStock` passes receiverWorkerId (code-only). **U8: worker-portal receipt confirm** — `/portal` รายการรอรับ + `PortalReceipts` (ได้รับแล้ว → `confirmStockIssue` → `confirm_stock_issue` RPC; U6 receiver-read RLS scopes the list to the bound worker); custody loop closed end-to-end (code-only). **U9: stock count / variance** — `stock_counts` (append-only, system_qty snapshot + counted_qty + generated variance/variance_value at moving-avg cost) + `record_stock_count` RPC (SITE_STAFF gate, reconciles on-hand to the counted truth; shrinkage = a store P&L hit; mig 20260809000400, pgTAP 184). **U10: count UI** — per-row `ตรวจนับ` button on /store (manager-tier) → counted-qty sheet with a live variance preview → `recordStockCount` (code-only). **U11: reversals** — `stock_reversals` (append-only, typed FK to receipt OR issue, unique per movement) + `reverse_stock_receipt` (BACK_OFFICE, guards on-hand ≥ receipt qty) / `reverse_stock_issue` (SITE_STAFF, adds qty/value back); mig 20260809000500, pgTAP 185. **U12: reversal UI** — /store รับเข้าล่าสุด list + `กลับรายการ` on receipts (any /store user) and issues (manager-tier) via `ConfirmActionButton` → `reverseStockReceipt`/`reverseStockIssue` (code-only). **Store cost-side COMPLETE (U1–U12).** wp_profit wiring · late margin layer · GL posting = later units |

| 211 | Procurement terminology & level clarity — operator "admins can't intuitively distinguish the PO from the PO Items." Backed by a multi-agent UX audit (`docs/procurement-uxui-audit-2026-06.md`, 85 surfaces / 91 verified findings). Root cause is structural: a PO has no status/total column (derived from member PRs), the whole area is named `คำขอซื้อ`, and the PR/PO status maps share `สั่งซื้อแล้ว`/`กำลังจัดส่ง`. Decomposed into SSOT units: **U1** split PO status vocabulary (ordered→`ออกใบสั่งซื้อแล้ว`, `in_transit`→`กำลังจัดส่งทั้งใบ`) + cross-map collision guard · U2 typed PO/PR id chips + `formatPoNumber`/`formatPrNumber` · U3 de-overload `รายการ` · U4 distinct PO pill + level captions · U5 PO membership in every band · U6 neutral nav landmark (`จัดซื้อ`) + breadcrumbs · U7 band-label SSOT · U8 notification PO-awareness (held) · U9 accounting trail (held) · U10 remaining terminology SSOT · U11 buy-action consolidation. Code-only; no schema |

| 212 | SA daily report (รายงานประจำวัน) — operator shared a real hand-typed LINE daily report; ~80% is data the app already holds (attendance = `labor_logs` spec 46, work = WP/deliverable, progress = `photo_logs`) being re-typed in a disconnected channel, while the genuine narrative (work summary / ปัญหาที่พบ / next-day plan) has no home. Makes the report a **byproduct of structured capture**: the app auto-assembles attendance-by-WP + the day's photos; the SA adds only narrative + submits; a PM confirms (รอยืนยัน → ยืนยันแล้ว); a "share as text" formatter reproduces the exact LINE message (the adoption bridge). One report per `(project, date)`; money-free (SA-tier); **no clock in/out** (sample times are boilerplate). U1 `daily_reports` table + RPCs · U2 auto-assembly read layer · U3 SA screen · U4 confirm · U5 share-text. AI-drafted narrative + DC self-report (spec 130) deferred |

| 222 | Supply plan — one item into multiple work packages — the แผนจัดหา grid (`SupplyPlanManager`, spec 181 U2) allocates each row to **one** WP (or `ทั้งโครงการ`), so planning the same material across several WPs means re-picking the item once per WP. Operator: don't force a WP (already true — `work_package_id` is nullable, save needs only item + qty) **and** let one item be picked into many WPs. Chose the **"pre-fill rows"** model: a per-row `＋ หลายงาน` checklist of WPs → confirm fans the row into **N draft rows** (same item, one WP each, blank qty) the planner fills, saved via the existing bulk write. **Code-only**, no schema/RPC change (`(plan,item,WP)` line + nullable WP + array insert already exist). **U1** the grid fan-out + `expandRowToWorkPackages` helper |

| 246 | SemVer release automation — **semantic-release** on push to `main`, driven by the enforced Conventional Commits (`fix:`→patch · `feat:`→minor · BREAKING→major; docs/test/refactor/chore → no release): bumps `package.json` (the app stamps `pkg.version` into feedback/telemetry `app_version`, so reports finally pin the real build), writes `CHANGELOG.md`, tags `vX.Y.Z`, GitHub Release, one `chore(release) [skip ci]` commit back (Vercel redeploys it). Baseline tag `v0.1.0`; stay 0.x until beta go-live → then a deliberate 1.0.0. | 264 | Staff self-onboarding (generalizes spec 263) — DRAFT, requires **ADR 0072** (supersedes ADR 0071). Turns the technician-specific self-registration into ONE **role-parametric internal-staff** self-onboarding flow: a `visitor` self-registers (identity + docs) → `pending` → an **approver assigns the role** (applicant does NOT self-select; optional free-text hint only) → mint universal `PRC-YY-NNNN` ID + e-card + set `users.role` + a **per-role side-effect** → land on that role's home. Technician = instance #1; procurement/accounting/hr/pc reuse the SAME table/queue/RPC (config, not code). **Rename** `technician_registrations`→`staff_registrations` (+attachments, enum `technician_doc_purpose`→`staff_doc_purpose` with `consent` dropped, `can_see_*`, all RPCs); `employee_id_counters` kept; open link `/register/technician` STAYS. **Role-parametric** `approve_staff_registration(p_id, p_role, p_project_id)` — `STAFF_ASSIGNABLE_ROLES` guard (internal only; NEVER visitor/contractor/client/`super_admin`), floor (`full_name` + live `id_card` + PDPA consent record), inline role flip, side-effect branch (`STAFF_FIELD_ROLES`=technician→`workers(worker_type='own')`; office roles→role-only, no workers row). **One-page form** (all fields + docs, optional beyond floor, LINE-avatar default; **PDPA consent = in-app record** `staff_consents`/`record_staff_consent`, `contractor_consents` pattern — replaces the consent file upload). **Kill /coming-soon dead-end**: new `/technician` home + `roleHome(technician)`→`/technician`; **visitor router** revamp of /coming-soon (pending `staff_registration` → register workspace · pending invite → claim page · organic → open CTA + invite note, loop-safe). **Role-neutral approval queue** + role selector at approve. **G1** schema (rename + parametric approve + consent record + assignable guard, held) · **G2** one-page form + technician→staff renames (auto) · **G3** /technician home + roleHome + visitor router (held) · **G4** role-neutral queue + role selector (auto). External subcon/client (Family B invite/claim) UNTOUCHED, no self-select hub. Out: office-role registration UI, `staff_members` office record, per-target-role approver policy, re-apply-after-reject, DC→technician merge. |

| 265 | Super-admin LINE-identity visibility (anti-impersonation / approval-time verification) — DRAFT, **no ADR** (additive to the identity model; extends ADR 0020's LINE-owned/refresh-on-login split). `super_admin` always sees an employee's LINE **ground-truth** identity — LINE display name + **original** LINE avatar + a "last checked" time — regardless of the person's edited `full_name` or uploaded `profile_photo`; becomes the approval-time verification signal for spec 264 (why no on-site SA vouch was built). Two new **nullable** `users` columns: **`line_display_name text`** (LINE-owned, refreshed EVERY login from `claims.name` — SEPARATE from the user-editable NULL-only `full_name`, so it never drifts) + **`line_synced_at timestamptz`** ("last checked" = last LINE login/sync; NO cron/worker). Callback (`auth/line/callback`) always sets both + existing avatar refresh, in the **existing step-7 profile-update block ONLY** — token exchange/`id_token` verify/session mint/CSRF-state/handoff/redirect UNTOUCHED (auth-outage fence); `full_name` NULL-only invariant preserved. `super_admin`-gated, server-side read (LINE avatar = plain `<img referrerPolicy=no-referrer>`, no `next/image`). Surfaces: identity block on `/registrations/[id]` (read via admin client scoped to the applicant's `user_id`) + a per-employee detail route **`/settings/roles/[id]`** (RLS session read; reuses the existing `super_admin` role-admin surface, mirrors `/settings/usage/[actorId]`). **U1** migration + surgical callback write + db:types + pgTAP (held: migration + auth file) · **U2** the two views (likely held: imports `src/lib/auth` gate). No backfill (lossy — `full_name` may be edited; `line_display_name` populates on next login, shows "ยังไม่ได้ซิงค์" until then). Open Q (recommended default in spec): also show the approval-time block to `procurement_manager`/`project_director` (YES). Out: periodic re-sync, on-demand re-check, cross-user avatar display, `full_name` semantics, `/sa/registrations` retitle. |

| 274 | `super_admin` "View as role" (ADR 0077) — ✅ COMPLETE (U1+U2+U3) 2026-07-07. `super_admin` renders the app AS any role (nav/home/page-gates/actions) while keeping its own identity + RLS — **view-as-role**, NOT user-impersonation; **fully active** (the assumed role gates actions). One pure resolver `resolveEffectiveRole(realRole, cookie)` (assumed IFF real=`super_admin` AND cookie ∈ `ASSUMABLE_ROLES`) wired into `loadUserContext` + `requireActionRole`; httpOnly `assumed_role` cookie. **Forge-guard**: a non-super's cookie is inert (real-role re-checked every request). **Fidelity ceiling** (ADR 0077): Postgres always sees `super_admin` via `current_user_role()` → no new privilege, writes run as `super_admin`, audit is TS-layer. Assumable = served roles with a UI (excl. `super_admin` + `/coming-soon`); identity-scoped roles (technician/contractor/client) show a "no personal data in this view" placeholder. **U1** ✅ resolver + cookie I/O + 2 gate wirings + enter/exit actions (real-role gated) + logout-clear + TDD · **U2** ✅ `getActiveViewAs` state helper + `/settings/view-as` picker (DetailHeader, real-role gated) + global exit banner in root layout + `ViewAsEmptyNote` on technician/portal/client (audit DEFERRED — no TS audit path) · **U3** ✅ `applyAssumedRole` helper + migrated 32 inline `select("role")` gate sites across 12 action files (labor/nova/projects/portal/reports/review/contacts) so the assumed role gates writes too — identity for non-super callers (zero real-user change). Code-only, no schema. Danger-path (`src/lib/auth/**`) → operator-held. |

Later: letter-in-WP-code generator. Design brief + letter/color/icon rationale in `277-work-category-visual-identity.md`. |

| 279 | Self-governance worker onboarding — the roster the spec 278 U2 attendance muster reads. `workers` was EMPTY firm-wide (spec 266 wipe) + `labor_logs` 0 all-time → the muster (and the whole ADR-0060 cost/profit engine that reads `labor_logs`) is dead-on-arrival → the real blocker is **worker onboarding**. Operator chose **self-governance**: each crew-lead onboards/manages their own crew (SA proxy-types for the phoneless), while the gameable money-adjacent attributes (`level`→sell-rate, pay-class, day-rate, tenure) stay **PM/PD/super-confirmed** (ADR 0060 §5 anti-self-dealing) and phoneless PII rests on a PDPA lawful basis (ADR 0061). 5 gated stages (identity → basis → classify/pay-ready → app-claim → lifecycle) — nobody incomplete leaks into cost/pay/muster. **U1** (this PR) = the crew spine: `crews` + `crew_members` (SSOT, one active crew per worker) + reuse `workers.tax_id` as the firm-wide dedup key + `create_crew`/`reassign_crew_lead` (gate `is_back_office`) + `current_user_led_crew_ids()` + RLS; lead authority = `current_user_worker_id() = lead_worker_id` predicate. U2 add-member+confirm · U3 consent (⚖️ counsel) · U4 lead surface+PM queue · U5 self-claim+lifecycle · U6 crew↔WP edge+cost gates. Danger-path epic, operator-held. ADR 0079. Design hole-checked by a 19-agent adversarial workflow. |

| 281 | แนะนำแผนพรุ่งนี้ — heuristic draft-board recommender for tomorrow's work. 🎨 DESIGN (approved 2026-07-08). Heuristic, not LLM (per ai-first-**prove-value** + no runtime): tiers **ต่อจากวันนี้** (aggressive carry-forward of every started-not-done leaf งาน, D3) → **ช้ากว่าแผน** (271 baseline slip) → **ลำดับความสำคัญ** (priority rank), each with a plain reason; crew pre-assigned by recent-continuity → 277 category-match → blank. **Default-selected, not forced** (D4): every row pre-checked, SA unchecks/edits, ใช้ที่เลือก commits via 273's RPCs; **nothing writes until approved** (D5). Code-only v1 (ephemeral draft, no schema). **U1** engine (`recommendTomorrowBoard`) · **U2** แนะนำ surface in `/sa/plan`. Phase-2 = LLM swap behind the same `DraftItem[]` contract. Complements spec **276** (which ensures a board exists). Detail in `281-tomorrow-work-recommender.md`. |

| 282 | SA site team board — at-a-glance on-site headcount + team structure. 🎨 DESIGN, **OPEN DECISIONS** (money-adjacent). Split out of spec-279 U6 because it is not a rendering change: **internal-vs-external is a property of the _arrangement_ (who pays/charges for a person's labor today), fluid per-engagement**, not a static team/person attribute (operator's two cross-charge reminders — our tech in a subcon crew = we charge; a subcon person as our day-labor = we pay). No clean data home today: two disjoint person-models (`workers`/`crews` vs `subcontract_crew_members`, unlinked), the true split lives in the empty/unbuilt labor-cost ledger (ADR 0060 money-v2), and the ฝ่ายไซต์ (SA+owner) bucket needs a `SECURITY DEFINER` read (`users` is own-row-only for an SA). **§3 = THE decision (A/B/C):** A team-nature approximation + `contractor_id`/`employment_type` exception badges (recommended v1, mostly code-only) · B arrangement-level billing-direction flag (true model, schema+ADR) · C defer the split, ship structure+headcount+site-access. Board = total + ทีมภายใน/ทีมภายนอก/ฝ่ายไซต์ buckets + expandable cards (reuses U6/U7b `buildCrewTeams`). Detail in `282-sa-site-team-board.md`. |

Level reuses `workers.level`; Position (generalize `ht_worker_id`) + reclassify `site_owner`/`subcon_manager`→Positions = phase 2; office-staff seam + dept-scoped RLS + permits/disputes = phase 3. | 342 | Invite-only office onboarding — operator 2026-07-22 ("track who invited, but also prevent new user picking wrong role"). There is no office invite path at all: `technicianOnboardUrl` mints only field/subcon links, so office hires arrive via a bare URL or the `/coming-soon` door, and live data shows `invited_by` **0/18** with the free-text `declared_role_hint` filled on only **7/18**. A reusable per-inviter link `/register/office?by=<uuid>&role=<key>` replaces both: the role renders to the applicant as read-only fact (no input to get wrong), `/coming-soon` drops the office door for a "ขอลิงก์เชิญ" line, a bare `/register/office` renders a gate screen (never a 404), and the approver's selector prefills from the invited role while still binding only on confirm. **NO schema** — `invited_by` and `declared_role_hint` already exist, and the URL-borne role is applicant-tamperable, which is exactly what that column already means. The `?by` check is uuid-shape only: UX, not a security boundary (a hard gate would be a DEFINER `is_staff_inviter()`, deliberately deferred). U1 mint on `/settings/roles` · U2 door + `registerLoginNext` role threading · U3 approver prefill. |
| 343 | Registration completion — prep screen + a pending state that tells the truth. Operator report 2026-07-22 ("users reported nowhere to give consent, nor uploads"), raised during contractor-team onboarding. Live data: **4 of 4** pending registrations hold `full_name` only — 0 `id_card`, 0 PDPA consent — two of them stuck 14 days after being personally chased, so this is not inattention. Three defects compound: the pending notice claims `ส่งใบสมัครแล้ว รอการอนุมัติ` the instant the profile saves (and its anti-phishing line `ไม่ต้องส่งบัตรให้ใครเพิ่ม` reads as _no card needed_); `StaffDocuments` + `StaffConsentCheckbox` render **after** the full-width primary CTA, below the fold; and the only outstanding-items hint is gated `{!floorMet && !consentedAt}`, so it vanishes the moment consent is ticked while an `id_card` is still owed. **NO schema.** A single all-required submit is impossible — `add_staff_registration_doc` requires the storage path's middle folder to equal `auth.uid()`, so the registration row must exist before any upload. U1 truthful pending state (reorder above CTA · floor-aware notice · un-gate the hint) · U2 เตรียมตัว prep screen as a state not a route (a separate page would re-thread `?project&site&by&contractor&firm`, the #677 bug class) · U3 `เตรียมบัตรประชาชนก่อนสแกน` on the poster + LINE share. Explicit non-goals, recorded: no office upload proxy, no consent proxy, no technician document deferral. |
| 345 | Accounting payment-document audit (ตรวจเอกสารการเงิน) — operator ask 2026-07-23: accounting admin reviews every payment document under every project, corrects fields, flags sum-mismatch / not-a-receipt asap; plus "plan for AI adoption". Live map: ~15 money-event families post to GL but only 5 carry documents (`pr-attachments`, `po-attachments`, `expense-attachments`, `rental-settlement-receipts`, subcontract header); 10 are doc-less, headed by `wage_payments` and `client_receipts`. Design = a generic review layer keyed `(source_table, source_id)` (the GL-outbox key shape): `money_event_reviews` (`pending|verified|flagged`, stale-verify triggers flip verified→pending on amount change) + append-only `money_review_flags` (flag types incl. `missing_doc`, `wrong_doc_type`, `amount_mismatch`, `sum_mismatch`, `duplicate_doc`; `raised_by_kind reviewer|agent|system`; status `suggested|open|resolved|dismissed`) · `/accounting/review` union queue + per-event voucher (docs beside entered fields) · flags route to the uploader via the notification outbox and resolve two-way (spec-201/324 shape) · corrections = direct by accounting via append/supersede paths, GL self-heals (client billings + WHT flag-only) · slip uploads added for `wage_payments` + `client_receipts` · period close soft-gated on outstanding counts. AI phased in: B = worker vision extraction (`money_review_doc_extractions`, one per object) raising `suggested` flags the admin confirms; C = per-source auto-verify behind a ≥95% agreement gate + operator toggle. Operator decisions recorded: direct-correct · layer+top-gaps · flags→uploader · soft-gate close. |
| 346 | Structured names — prefix · first · last · nickname, with the nickname becoming the app's display name. Operator ask 2026-07-22/23 during onboarding ("first and last name should not be in the same field, prefix should be selection", "+ nickname", "Option C … let admin fix that until we have a clean database"). Live: a name lives in ONE free-text `full_name` on `users`/`staff_registrations`/`crew_registrations`; **no** structured column exists; `users.full_name` is a LINE display handle (40 rows, 22 single-token, 0 prefixed) so it CANNOT be split; nicknames already ARE the working identity, jammed into `contractors.name` parens. Option C = parts become the truth, **filled by admins not by a lossy split**, `full_name` kept as fallback until a clean DB then dropped. Prefix = a `name_prefixes` LOOKUP table (house pattern, not an enum). Display resolves nickname → composed legal → legacy `full_name`; legal surfaces (contracts/payroll/WHT) use composed-legal only. U1 schema (lookup + 4 nullable columns) · U2 registration form (prefix select + first + last + nickname) · U3 `displayName`/`legalName` helper at the top naming surfaces · U4 admin cleanup surface + NULL→value direct-fill RPC (never value→value/value→NULL) · U5 approval arm for already-set names **BLOCKED on a crux** (the identity-change flow is `full_name`-string-based — extend it with structured columns vs compose-and-re-split, operator call) · U6 retire `full_name` (gated on a clean DB). ⭐ numbered 346 because 344+345 were taken by concurrent lanes mid-design. |
| 348 | `procurement_manager` = `site_admin` superset (SA parity, see-all) — operator ask 2026-07-23: "procurement manager needs all accesses that SA can, she needs to explain to them how to use each feature." Brainstorm locked FULL parity (do-everything — a TS-only lens renders SA screens with empty lists + dead buttons, useless for teaching) + SEE-ALL scope (`can_see_project` see-all arm, like `project_director`). ADR 0084; extends ADR 0070's superset doctrine to the SA tier. U1 DB read parity (`can_see_project` + `is_site_staff` + SELECT-policy literal arms + `audit_log` reader allowlist) · U2 TS read surfaces (`SITE_STAFF_ROLES` += `procurement_manager` w/ consumer audit, page gates, guard pins updated deliberately) · U3 DB write parity (live-sweep inventory ~44 fns + INSERT/UPDATE policies + 2 `storage.objects` arms: WP lifecycle, photos, labor, muster 6-pack, store/stock, site purchase, SA worker add + bank capture, site issues, equipment) · U4 `isReadOnlyWpViewer` → procurement only (WP detail goes full-affordance for `procurement_manager`) · U5 teaching lens (spec-274 view-as gains a per-assumer map — `procurement_manager` may assume `site_admin` ONLY; ships strictly AFTER U3 so the lens is DB-backed, inverting spec 274's safety argument) · U6 ADR + docs. Rules: SA parity is a ceiling not a floor (arms that deliberately exclude `site_admin` — e.g. the client-source defect arm — exclude her too); plain `procurement` byte-unchanged; money model untouched; team-add picker unchanged (membership is a no-op under see-all). |

| 349 | Accounting nav redesign — work-queue-first, 4 destination tabs. Operator ask 2026-07-23 ("redesign nav system for accounting staffs"). Grounded in 30d telemetry (3 users): `/settings/company-docs` = 100 hits (#1 real destination, buried 2 taps), `/accounting/purchases` 30, `/accounting/review` 0 (spec 345 queue shipped 07-22, one link among 9 on the GL landing). `ACCOUNTING_TABS`/`ACCOUNTING_HUB_NAV` 2→4: งานตรวจ `/accounting/review` (new roleHome) · บัญชี `/accounting` · เอกสาร `/settings/company-docs` · ตั้งค่า. Section-spine grammar rejected (would ratify the 2nd grammar while audit Decision 2 is open). D4: `super_admin` covered by `SETTINGS_TAB.match` `/accounting` (the 313-U5 stranding class, checked at design time). D5: MA business unit ruled same-legal-entity → non-goal, zero schema prep. U1 tabs+strip (code-only) · U2 roleHome flip + review hub chrome (danger-held) · U3 `/accounting` home trim. NO schema. Serialize behind lane 348parity (same role-set SSOTs). |
| 347 | Store-first purchase price correction (แก้ไขราคาซื้อผ่านสโตร์) — the stock-layer price fix spec 345 U4 deferred: `correct_purchase_amount` as a plain `PR.amount` UPDATE would repost GL for ~0% of real purchases (536/540 money-event PRs are store-first/wp-null; their money books as INVENTORY off the `stock_receipt`, and the purchase GL trigger requires `work_package_id IS NOT NULL`). Design = spec 324's VALUE sibling: append-only `stock_receipt_price_corrections` (signed deltas net/vat/gross, composing) + `correct_purchase_price(receipt, corrected_gross, corrected_vat_rate, reason, flag?)` gated `is_back_office OR accounting`, fresh-pool-guarded (480/485 live receipts qualify; issued tail refused with guidance), mutually exclusive with the qty-correction/reversal family BOTH directions. GL contra keeps the 1500↔on-hand tie (a wrong price moves both sides equally, so NO integrity check can see it — only the 345 review does). **`PR.amount` is never updated** (load-bearing: the receipt poster reads gross from it at drain time; immutability makes re-post + contra compose instead of double-correct) — readers show effective amount = `PR.amount` + Σ `delta_gross` + a แก้ไขราคาแล้ว chip. Explicit 345 tie-in: correction flips the purchase review verified→pending + resolves the applying flag. Operator calls locked 2026-07-23: fresh-pool-only v1 · stock-correction role set · immutable PR.amount + effective-amount read layer · `vat_rate` corrects together. U1 ledger+ALL GL plumbing (poster, drain arm, integrity pending-lists) BEFORE U2 RPC+reciprocal guards · U3 voucher แก้ไขราคา door · U4 effective-amount read sweep (dashboard = classify-not-build; stock/GL-derived readers are already correct). |

| 350 | Technician progress view (งานที่ได้รับมอบหมาย) — operator direction 2026-07-23 ("technicians should do more stuff related to them, Nova model"); first anchor chosen over the attendance/money asks (those sit on the deeper spec-306 U5 money spine). Fills the `ComingSoonBadge` placeholder that has stood on `/technician` since 264 G3 / 266 U7. A ช่าง sees their most-recent muster team's work — code + name + status pill + progress (งาน → `X% (n/m เสร็จ)`; งานย่อย → status + parent %). Data path: `auth.uid()` → `workers.user_id` → latest `muster_attendance` → `muster_team_wps`; WP progress is `status`-derived (no numeric column) + `deriveDeliverableProgress` group rollup. Needs a self-scoped read RPC because muster RLS is project-membership (`can_see_project`), which a technician fails. Read-only (no status edits, no actions — ADR 0074 keeps งาน oversight-only); scope-guarded to progress only (no attendance/money/OT; no persistent assignment; no self-check-in). U1 `get_my_assigned_work()` DEFINER read RPC + pgTAP (schema — operator-merged) · U2 `/technician` card + progress view-model + vitest (code-only). |
| 351 | Separate OT muster session (แยกเช็คชื่อ OT) — operator direction 2026-07-23: a technician's normal hours (08:00–17:00) and OT (17:30–whenever) are tracked as TWO separate clock-in/out sessions, not one evening scan against a 17:00 threshold. Reworks the LIVE spec-306 muster schema (forward-only, 13 rows, all pre-rework `regular`): `muster_session` enum + `session` column on `muster_attendance` + composite `unique(worker_id,work_date,session)`; `muster_scan_in`/`_out` gain `p_session` (OT-in guarded on a regular session existing on the same team first; `ot_hours` becomes the OT session's real span, null on regular); `close_muster_day` auto-outs `regular` only (OT "till whenever" left open + cockpit-flagged); `move_muster_worker` moves ALL of the worker's sessions. Supersedes 306 U4's threshold-OT model; hands the two session types to 306 U5 (money, still operator-held — no OT rate, WP attribution, or 17:30 pay-clamp here). U1 schema + reworked RPCs + pgTAP (schema — operator-merged) · U2 cockpit evening/OT flow + two-session loader + OT-span display (code-only). |
| 352 | ถอนงานกลับมาแก้ไข: recall a submitted WP to fix its evidence — operator directive 2026-07-24 ("WO-04-47 images placed wrongly, delete them then let me do it without help"). Photo removal on a `pending_approval` WP is frozen (291/340 freeze); this builds the self-serve path to pull a WP back to `in_progress`, fix evidence, resubmit. U1 schema + `can_recall_work_package` RPC (mig `075847`) · U2 code. |
| 353 | WP rejection paths + หลังแก้ไข availability — operator directive 2026-07-24 ("หลังแก้ไข should only be available when rework is needed; PM has 2 rejections: reject evidence and reject work"). Code-only, 4 units, NO schema (operator chose UI + server-action gate over a DB trigger). The `showAfterFix` gate (`status='rework' || after_fix.length>0`) conflates capture-vs-history and drives the **capture** tile, and the `photo_logs` INSERT policy has **no status gate** on a fresh photo — so the หลังแก้ไข shutter appears AND writes on non-rework WPs (live: **21 WPs**, 20 complete + 1 `pending_approval`, all `rework_round=0`). Fix: split the gate; `after_fix` CAPTURE window = `rework_round>0 && (status='rework' || isRevisionWindowOpen)` (reuses the deletion-window SSOT; handles a reworked WP bounced with `needs_revision`), keeping the read-only history strip so nothing is lost. Plus: mirror the gate in `addPhoto`; sharpen the two rejection labels on the evidence-vs-work axis + fix the stale SA-facing `APPROVAL_DECISION_LABEL.rejected="ไม่อนุมัติ"` drift + unify the two label homes; align the resubmit gate to the current evidence phase. RPC untouched. U1 split gate + history strip · U2 action gate · U3 framing + label SSOT · U4 resubmit alignment. Detail in `353-afterfix-rejection-paths.md` + plan in `353-afterfix-rejection-paths-plan.md`. |
| 354 | Image capture-method telemetry — operator question 2026-07-24 ("are images taken in-app or uploaded more?"). Today unanswerable: no per-image method is recorded and EXIF is stripped on downscale, so even the 2410-image `photos` bucket (which accepts camera shutter + a spec-96 gallery button + defect picker, all mixed) can't be split. Adds the missing signal WITHOUT schema: stamp `{ metadata: { captureMethod } }` — `camera` (`capture="environment"`, provable) / `library` (gallery button) / `picker` (plain `accept`, ambiguous) — into `storage.objects.user_metadata` at every image `.upload()` across all buckets (supabase-js 2.105 + `user_metadata` col both confirmed live). One SSOT `src/lib/photos/capture-method.ts`; one read query. Known limit: `picker` = affordance not sensor (camera-vs-gallery only true on `camera`/`library` surfaces). U0 spike (prove deployed client persists metadata) · U1 SSOT + WP paths (incl. offline IDB queue item) · U2 purchasing · U3 expenses · U4 catalog/feedback · U5 contacts/portal/profile/register · U6 report query. No schema, no backfill (existing ~2900 = unknown), no behaviour change. |
| 356 | Delete a progress photo from the WP-detail photo viewer — operator report 2026-07-24 ("SA/operator cannot delete images" on an editable WP). Deletion IS permitted (`canDeleteWpPhotos` true, `removePhoto` admits SA/PM/PD/super, RLS agrees) — but the `ลบรูป` overlay button is only wired inside the CaptureSheet; tapping a photo on the WP-detail page opens the same overlay view-only. Fix = thread the EXISTING `canDelete` (already on the page) + an `onDeletePhoto` (→ `removePhoto` + `router.refresh`) into `PhotoCaptureZone`'s current-phase + read-only หลังแก้ไข-history `ZoomablePhoto` strips; reuses the overlay's confirm + the 291/340/341 gate, numbering and trace (revalidated on delete). PM defect photos + defect-pair thumbnails stay view-only (SA fixes, not deletes). Decision (surfaced): keep the zone-level-`canDelete` offer-then-refuse in the ให้แก้ไข window, matching the CaptureSheet (threading per-photo `uploaded_by` = deferred rough edge). Code-only, single unit, NO schema. Detail in `356-wp-detail-photo-delete.md`. |
| 355 | Structured reject-evidence reasons (why the PM sends photos back) — operator directive 2026-07-24, follow-on from spec 353. The wrong-WP upload problem surfaces at review as **PM rejections caused by image mismatch** ("รูปไม่ตรงกับงาน"). Grounded in the 37 live `needs_revision` comments: three cause families — **incomplete** (add missing phase photos, the bulk) · **mismatch** (wrong photos → remove + re-shoot; the wrong-WP pain caught at review; the wrong-photo family is ~¼ of distinct reasons) · **premature** (submitted too early → finish first). Spec 353 gives all three the generic "ถ่ายรูปใหม่", which is wrong for mismatch (add vs remove-and-replace) and premature. Fix: a REQUIRED structured reason on reject-evidence (new enum `approval_revision_reason` + `approvals.revision_reason` col + `decide_work_package` `p_revision_reason`, validated non-null iff `needs_revision`; comment demotes to optional detail), each driving a TAILORED SA next-action on the spec-353 attention card (mismatch → remove-the-wrong-photos + re-shoot, surfacing the spec-291 delete window). Makes mismatch **measurable** (the yardstick for later capture-time prevention, parked). reject-work untouched. No photo-MOVE across WPs (deferred). U1 schema (enum+col+RPC, additive mig) · U2 PM reason chips · U3 SA tailored guidance. Detail in `355-revision-reasons.md` + plan in `355-revision-reasons-plan.md`. |
| 357 | Muster cockpit redesign — 6 operator usability points 2026-07-24 after the day-1 pilot muster. (1) QR moves to a header icon per team card + (3) `+ เพิ่มช่าง` removed with tap-add RE-HOMED into the scan sheet (scan OR tap — the phoneless/lost-badge path survives; signal-removal rule) · (2) WP picker shows only incomplete leaves (`status != 'complete'`; assigned-but-completed stays visible with an เสร็จแล้ว tag — #742 invariant) and default-selects the prior muster day's still-incomplete set for the same lead (plan = pre-fill, save = truth) · (4) ย้าย removed (no intra-day team change; `move_muster_worker` RPC stays; "team change at OT" = flagged future feature) · (6) ยังไม่มา section = live `crew_members` of the lead's active crews MINUS today's check-ins, tap-to-check-in · (5) `workers.gender` (new nullable enum + column-grant widen + `p_gender` on create/`update_worker` + roster form field + ช/ญ chip) — danger-path, LAST. Units U-E/U-D/U-B/U-C code-only · U-F schema (operator-merged). Detail in `357-muster-cockpit-redesign.md` + plan in `357-muster-cockpit-redesign-plan.md`. |
| 358 | Attendance audit for office / payroll (ประวัติการเช็คชื่อ) — operator direction 2026-07-25: office staff (accounting/HR first, PM/PD too) need to AUDIT attendance — history, not just today: per-worker days present + OT hours over a month/date range, per-day in/out detail, the audit signals (`in_method` qr|manual, `out_auto`, `scanned_by`, closure state), cross-project, exportable for payroll. No such surface exists (only the today-locked `/projects/:id/muster` cockpit + the `/team` hero card). Access = NEW `SECURITY DEFINER` read RPCs gated on a NEW `ATTENDANCE_AUDIT_ROLES` set (`{accounting, hr, project_director, project_coordinator, procurement_manager, super_admin, project_manager}`) with the full-set = cross-project and PM = `can_see_project`-scoped — RLS untouched; `is_back_office` EXCLUDES accounting/hr so it's the wrong gate (spec-350 read-RPC pattern). Route `/team/attendance` (DetailHeader + `?from` back chip, entry cards on /team + /accounting, no role-home flip). Shape = per-worker summary rows + per-day drill + CSV export; RAW scan truth only, hard boundary from money (no `labor_logs`/wages/GL). Live signal from row one: 0/22 QR (all manual), 9 open check-outs, 07-24 never closed. U1 RPCs+pgTAP (schema) · U2 page+range picker+per-worker rows · U3 per-day drill+signals · U4 CSV export · U5 cockpit past-day picker DEFERRED (357 cockpit-churn constraint). Detail in `358-attendance-audit.md` + plan in `358-attendance-audit-plan.md`. |
| 359 | Continuous QR sweep + camera-first muster add sheet — operator ask 2026-07-26 ("influence them to check in via QR, rather than SA manually checking them in"). Grounded live: QR is **1 of 36 real SA check-ins ever** (07-25 07:57:42, จันทร์ เงางาม, by Wutpong — his next 3 were manual: one trial then abandonment), 0 of 14 check-outs. Root cause is NOT worker motivation and a coin reward cannot reach it: **a worker cannot self-scan at all** — `muster_scan_in` gates `site_admin|super_admin|procurement_manager` and the badge payload is an opaque `workers.id` that `badge-qr.ts` says "authenticates nobody", so QR-vs-manual is purely the SA's input choice on the SA's device. The add sheet CLOSES on a successful decode while the tap list stays open across taps ⇒ a 14-person team costs 14 door-opens + 14 camera acquisitions by scan vs 1 + 14 taps; scanning is structurally slower than the path it competes with. Measured from `in_at` gaps: **1–6s per person within a team, 22s per team switch.** Design = the deferral `muster-add-sheet.tsx` itself names ("continuous multi-scan is deferred until the #745 decode loop has on-device proof"). **U1** sheet stops closing on decode + per-badge ~3s cooldown (the 180ms loop would otherwise fire ~5 scans/s off one lingering badge) + audible/haptic per-outcome cues (the SA watches the line, not the screen) + newest-first tally + **6 outcomes none of which tear down the camera** (incl. "in another team today" demoted from a sheet-dumping error to a tally row with `ย้ายมาทีมนี้` offered after the sweep) + **team-change-vs-last-muster amber warn** (operator ask; compares last MUSTERED day not calendar yesterday, warns never blocks, roster looked up once at sheet-open) + pinned `กำลังเช็คเข้า · ทีมX · งานปกติ` action header — load-bearing because `scanFromCamera` dispatches on the เข้า/ออก + regular/OT toggles and a continuous sweep in ออก mode would check a whole team OUT in fifteen seconds, silently. **U2** camera-first default with the tap list behind a `ไม่มีบัตร / หาไม่เจอ` disclosure (no-camera devices unchanged), shipping ONLY after U1's QR share moves — camera-first before that hides the SA's only working path behind a disclosure. ⚠️ on-device proof for the jsQR loop is still a SINGLE scan (device + decode path unrecorded); a validation morning is owed, and if jsQR stalls U1 degrades to continuous-on-native-detector / one-shot-on-jsQR and U2 does not ship. Metric = `muster_attendance.in_method` share (no new telemetry; `interaction_events` has no scan event). Code-only, NO schema. Rejected: team-agnostic scanner (operator: teams line up separately, the arrival-order pattern is a practice being replaced) · "same as yesterday" bulk prefill (turns attendance into a copy of yesterday) · worker-facing coins (wrong actor — a streak earn-rule on `coin_postings` rewards attendance, not scanning, and is a separate spec) · SA/office self-check-in (`muster_attendance.worker_id` FKs `workers`; office staff have no row — own spec). Open: QR check-out (17:00 auto-out may suffice) · whether the OT session gets the sweep. Detail in `359-continuous-qr-muster.md`. |
| 361 | Procurement master-data hub (ข้อมูลหลัก) + rental catalog — operator 2026-07-26, two asks in one: _"procurement manager requested to see rental details, accounting also needs to know, there is no information to input, do we have rental catalog similar to materials?"_ then _"procurement manager can edit catalogs full CRUD … group catalog settings for her"_. Live: `equipment_rental_batches` (29) records supplier + rate + dates and **no item at all** — the "what" survives only in free-text `note` (`เช่ารถแม็คโคร PC 140`, `รถ6ล้อดั้ม`, several blank), every rental journal entry's memo is the literal `Equipment rental`, and there is no `/accounting` rentals register. The taxonomy is already half-built and empty: `equipment_categories` holds `รถขุด` + `รถหกล้อ` with 0 items, and `equipment_items.rental_agreement_id` is a dead column (0 rows, 0 callers). Reference-data audit found three more gaps behind the operator's four: `office_expense_categories` (8) is **SELECT-only — no RPC, no UI**; `work_categories` (52) has CRUD RPCs with **zero callers** whose gate **refuses `procurement_manager`**; the managed unit list `catalog_units` (25, spec 223) has a live picker and RPCs that **already admit `procurement_manager`** but **zero callers**, so nobody can curate it in-app while the retained `อื่น ๆ (ระบุเอง)` hatch leaks **18 off-list strings onto 33 of 594 items** incl. the typo `ปิีป`. Decisions: rentals get their OWN small catalog (`rental_catalog_items`) — not `catalog_items` (stock semantics it never has) and not `equipment_items` (owned-asset registry) — sharing the existing equipment taxonomy; hub is a `/procurement` section (her home since 323 U4), not a new settings section; full CRUD for her except ราคาขาย (exec) and ประเภทเอกสารบริษัท (super, spec 331); only TOP-LEVEL work-category codes are locked (a refute-first fact-check killed the stronger "codes are immutable" rule — nothing FKs on `code` and spec 336 D4 calls the WP code a suggestion; the real hazard is `identity.ts` resolving `left(code,3)` against a hard-coded list, spec 277); the GL account on an expense category is OPTIONAL (all 8 live rows have it NULL and `office_expenses` posts no journal entries at all); the rental item name reaches the GL memo (`Equipment rental` × 30, verbatim, today). U1 rental catalog schema (`075857`) · U2 deal-form picker + item on every rental surface · U3 GL memo + `/accounting/rentals` · U4 the hub · U5 expense-category CRUD · U6 equipment-category rename (UI only) + deactivate (`is_active`) · U7 work-category CRUD + gate widening · U8 unit-curation UI over the existing `catalog_units` RPCs (code-only). Detail in `361-procurement-master-data-hub.md`. |

| 362 | Registry UI parity — operator 2026-07-26, verbatim: _"UI of ทะเบียนวัสดุ is better than ทะเบียนอุปกรณ์ and ทะเบียนค่าแรง"_; he then scoped it himself (ค่าแรง = **both** `/settings/labor-rates` and `/workers`; **full port, อุปกรณ์ first**). Diagnosis: `/catalog` is **READ-first** (search · filter chips with counts · grouped `label (n)` sections · card rows · every write inside a `BottomSheet` · empty + no-result states · `text-body`/`text-meta` tokens) while the other three are **FORM-first** — `/equipment` opens on **four** curator blocks (2 quick-adds, the spec-361 taxonomy card, a fully expanded add form) above **64 items in 9 categories** with no search/filter/grouping and `text-xs` inline-expanding row actions; `/settings/labor-rates` renders its **4** level rows as permanently open forms (no read state) under an `h1` that drifted to `text-lg font-semibold`; `/workers` puts the app's largest form (12 fields) above **29** ช่าง grouped only by `pay_type`, no search, no counts. This spec makes the วัสดุ shape house style and ports it. **NO schema, NO gate change** — role sets, server actions, RPCs, RLS and the money audiences (`dailyRates`, sell rates) carry across unchanged and are pinned by test. U1 `/equipment` (search + category chips + grouped cards; add/quick-add/edit/move → sheets) · U2 `/settings/labor-rates` (read-first rows + edit sheet + WHT heading + h1 token; no search — 4 fixed rows) · U3 `/workers` (search + การจ่าย chips + cards; add/edit → sheets). Non-goals recorded: no new fields, no sort control, no re-gating, `/catalog` untouched. Detail in `362-registry-ui-parity.md`. |

| 363 | WP detail — the site admin's navigation. Operator directive 2026-07-26 (7 points; points 6–7 = zones, split out as spec 366). The SA's six tabs become **three** — `รูปถ่าย · ของ · ประวัติ` — grounded on prod 2026-07-27 SA writes/30d: photos **2031**, WP-page purchase requests **143**, withdrawals **32** (all-time, 5 WPs), self-purchase **4 with 0 evidence**, `labor_logs` **0 all-time**. `ของ` is **item-first, not path-first**: one `ต้องการของ` action over a state-grouped list (`รออนุมัติ` / `อยู่ที่งานนี้` / `คืนแล้ว`), with the store's on-hand deciding whether `เบิก` or `ขอซื้อ` is the primary button — store-first becomes the path of least resistance instead of a chip the SA must notice. `ประวัติ` is one timeline (status · decisions inline · photo bursts collapsed · requests · เบิก · คืน) built with a **plan lane that renders nothing** (operator: no dates on the SA screen yet — 220 of 350 leaf WPs are already past `planned_end`). `ข้อมูล` folds into an unconditional ⓘ sheet; `แรงงาน` leaves the SA set but stays on the planner's `จัดการ`; เครื่องมือ gets its own foot-of-tab entry, activating the never-built spec 202 U2 (`equipment_usage_logs` = 0). U1 ⓘ · U2 timeline · U3 แรงงาน · U4 ของ · U5 sticky tabs + state bottom bar · U6 self-purchase evidence · U7 equipment. NO schema. Detail in `363-wp-detail-sa-nav.md`. |

| 366 | WP zones — photos that know where they were taken. Operator directive 2026-07-26, points 6–7 (the half [spec 363](363-wp-detail-sa-nav.md) deliberately left; ⚠️ 363 called this "364", which another lane claimed the same day). **Zones are an evidence axis, NOT a WP restructure** (D1): the WP tree is load-bearing for money — `labor_logs` bind to LEAF WPs, budgets and `wp_profit` derive per WP, and the single baseline has 331 items keyed to today's WPs. Live pain: **2,672 photos across 252 WPs, max 86 on one**, organised on ONE axis (`phase`), plus 4 `mismatch` rejections in the three days since spec 355 shipped. The concept already exists as unstructured TEXT — `...ภายในอาคาร` vs `...ภายนอกอาคาร`, `งานพื้นลานด้านหน้า/ด้านข้าง/ด้านหลัง`. Model: `project_drawings` (new private bucket) → `project_zones` (polygon as **normalised 0–1 JSONB, no PostGIS**) → `wp_zones`, plus nullable `photo_logs.zone_id` (set at capture, corrected only by supersede — the block-update/delete triggers and INSERT/SELECT-only grants were verified live). Zone at capture is **always optional** (operator call, recorded against the recommendation) but **sticky per session** (D5) — one tap per site visit. Templates = **clone a reference project** (D7) mirroring `clone_work_packages`, NOT a registry: `wp_templates` has 28 rows and zero readers anywhere. ⚠️ §1.1 names the failure to avoid — spec 248 built `answers_photo_id` and it holds **0 of 2,672** because it needed a curatorial act first; zones need one too, so U5 (clone) and the §8 fill-rate query are load-bearing, not nice-to-have. U1 schema · U2 PM authoring · U3 SA capture · U4 gallery + tap-a-polygon · U5 clone. Detail in `366-wp-zones.md`. |

| 365 | Team-map redesign: mirror the daily report's shape, surface the gaps — operator directive 2026-07-27, following #766/#776 (`procurement_manager` now owns crew management, project membership and the SA หลัก on this page). ⚠️ Numbered 365, not 364 — spec 363's own row reserves 364 for its zones split-out; grepping merged README rows (not just LANES/`ls docs/feature-specs`) is what caught the collision before shipping. The report groups people as ทีมภายใน (crews, PRC pays daily) → ผู้รับเหมาช่วง (contractors) → สนับสนุน (project staff); the page instead grouped staff by role tier and crews/firms/unassigned into one bucket — the mismatch let ทีมป้าสังวาลย์'s 5 members scatter across whichever team was open on 2026-07-26, since her team had no crew row to be picked into. CODE-ONLY, no schema — a regroup + a read-only fix-list layer over existing writes; every RPC/gate this page already uses is untouched. Model: three bands in report order (ทีมภายใน caption "จ้างรายวันโดย PRC" · ทีมภายนอก · ผู้รับเหมาช่วง, zero-worker firms get a two-door dashed card (existing spec-328 QR poster + existing `/workers` add) · สนับสนุน flattened to one list, no PM/SA sub-tiers); the day-plan layer demotes to its own tab (label carries the day's unassigned-team count). Fix-list = exactly 3 conditions 100%-derivable from existing tables (unassigned workers · a crew with `lead_worker_id is null` · an active contractor with 0 named workers) — explicitly NOT a fourth "firm the report names but has no DB row at all" condition (ห้องเย็น/ช่างไฟ/แม่บ้าน today), which is undetectable from a query; that case gets a standing `+ เพิ่มผู้รับเหมาช่วง` band-header door to `/contacts`'s existing create-firm flow instead. U1 regroup+rename · U2 fix-list · U3 plan-tab split. Detail in `365-team-map-report-shape-redesign.md`. |

| 367 | Equipment registry completeness + bulk import/export — operator directive 2026-07-27, three asks (field-gap analysis · import/export on both lists with fill-in templates · "we will export all equipments to PRI, a sister company, then we rent back the whole store"). Started from _"how do I see all the equipments in the store?"_, whose answer is the origin: `equipment_items` holds **64 rows** but `equipment_movements` holds **1 row total**, and location is derived purely from the latest movement ⇒ **63 of 64 render `—`**; nothing was ever recorded `รับเข้าคลัง`. The operator's hypothesis (equipment mis-registered under ทะเบียนเช่าอุปกรณ์) is **refuted** — `equipment_rental_batches` (29) is the inbound-agreement list and carries no item dimension at all; a **stale settings hint** (`hint: "ทะเบียนอุปกรณ์เช่า"` on the `/equipment` card) is what made it look otherwise. Fill rates say the rest: `acquisition_cost` **0/64**, `acquired_at` **0/64**, `daily_rate` **0/64**, `asset_tag` 5/64, `quantity` 9/64, while brand/model/serial are crammed into free-text `name` and **image, condition, description, disposal state and book value do not exist as columns at all** (`catalog_items` has `image_path` + a `catalog-images` bucket; equipment has neither). **D1 (operator ruling): ONE registry** — keep `equipment_items` as the single asset list and, on the PRI transfer, flip ownership and revive the dead `equipment_items.rental_agreement_id`; this **narrows spec 361 U1's `rental_catalog_items` to external rentals only** rather than duplicating all 64 rows. The PRI sale/rent-back itself is explicitly NOT built here — but it is why the fields land first: the export **is** the transfer schedule, and every value needed to price it is `0/64` today. U0 stale hint + 4 mis-filed items · U1 schema (6 columns, `equipment_condition` enum, `equipment_status += disposed`, `equipment-images` bucket, plus promoting the component-local `STATUS_LABELS`/tracking maps into the `labels.ts` SSOT the importer needs) · U2 both exports (Thai headers + UTF-8 BOM, `<a download>` not `Link`, money columns re-gated to `BACK_OFFICE_ROLES`) · U3 equipment importer (papaparse, CSV **or** Google-Sheets tab-paste, blank id = insert / filled id = update, preview-then-commit, unknown category/owner = error not auto-create) · U4 rental importer · U5 per-item image control. Acceptance is a **fill-rate query**, not a green suite. Detail in `367-equipment-registry-completeness.md`. |
| 368 | Equipment in the project store, and the site move — operator directive 2026-07-28 correcting spec 367's premise: the 64 tools "are supposed to be items in the store of the active project โพธิ์ทอง", split from materials, and all of it moves to a new site soon. **D1: tools stay in `equipment_items`; the STORE gains a view** — moving them into stock was rejected on evidence (all 10+ stock routines enumerated: there is NO project-to-project stock transfer, so stock-shaped tools would be stranded when โพธิ์ทอง closes; `equipment_movements.deployed` already carries `project_id` and the site move is one more movement). U1 ✅ 2026-07-28 (#812): pure `equipmentAtProject` + read-only `เครื่องมือและอุปกรณ์` section on `/projects/[id]/store` grouped by category, plus the data op backfilling 63 missing `deployed` movements (the original "everything renders `—`" bug — 64/64 now resolve to `PRC-2026-004`). U2 bulk site move (what directive point 2 actually asks for; needs the new site as a project) · U3 decide what `equipment_items` is for (PRI framing re-opened) · U4 the SA operating view, designed + approved 2026-07-28: the section splits `ยืมไปที่งาน` (open `equipment_usage_logs` anti-join — never `status`, which movements clobber) / `อยู่ในคลัง` (bulk always here, ×qty), oldest loan first with `ยืม n วัน` + who, and **คืน at the store** via the live `check_in_equipment` RPC gated `EQUIPMENT_MOVE_ROLES` (verified == the RPC's own allowlist); ยืม deliberately NOT here — WP-side (363 U7) or scan-first ([spec 370](370-equipment-scan-in-out.md)). ⚠️ this file was briefly the second "368" — the man-day spec landed an index row as 368 the same day and was renumbered to 369 (store keeps 368: elder merge + `Spec 368 U1` pinned in three `src/` store files). Detail in `368-store-equipment-view.md`. |
| 369 | WP labour budgets in man-days, surplus to Nova coins — implements ADR 0085, from the operator's 2026-07-28 proposal to budget internal WPs in coins. **The headline is a root cause, not a feature:** ADR 0060's entire economy shipped in June (specs 160/161/162 — ledger, dials, `wp_profit`, `settle_project`, `distribute_project_coins`, shop, vesting, `/nova` console) and is **completely unfed** — `coin_postings` 0 · `shop_items` 0 · `wp_economics` 0 · `labor_logs` 0 · `workers.day_rate` **฿0.00 on all 31** · `wp_labor_costs` 140 rows all ฿0.00 · 9 dials still placeholders. Cause, read live: `derive_muster_labor` requires `cost_confirmed_at is not null and day_rate > 0`, and the only RPC that stamps it — `confirm_worker_cost` (`super_admin`, derives the rate from `worker_level_rates` per ADR 0082) — **has no caller anywhere in `src/`**. `/workers` can set a level and can set a rate, but neither confirms. One missing button starves every economic feature already built. The inputs are ready: `worker_level_rates` holds human-entered ฿400/500/600/650 (2026-07-15) and `muster_attendance` is live (123 rows). U1 the cost-confirm door on `/workers` (`super_admin`-only, shows the rate it will stamp; acceptance = a fill-rate query, not a green suite) · U2 operator grades the **16 PRC-paid daily** workers, then a closed muster day writes the first `labor_logs` · U3 measure the real labour:material mix + back-simulate surplus on finished WPs (**no coin minted before this reports**) · U4 `labor_budget` entry + the field-facing man-day view (no baht on a field screen) · U5 surplus→coins at WP completion (`coin_postings.work_package_id`, 2 dials, a DEFINER RPC, `coin_source` enum-add — trips the exhaustiveness guards on purpose) · U6 worker-facing view **HELD** on ADR 0061 gift-first. Detail in `369-man-day-wp-budgets.md`. |
| 370 | Scan equipment in/out (QR + NFC) with condition photos — operator directive 2026-07-28 extending [spec 368](368-store-equipment-view.md) U4: _"Prepare for scanning items in/out (nfc/qr with backup method). Scanning opens up a camera to take picture(s) of equipment condition"_; backup _"search can be with names or s/n"_. **D1 scan is the primary door BOTH directions at the store** (revises the "ยืม WP-side only" lock — the physical handoff happens at the store; 363 U7 stays the browse door). **D2 identity = item uuid in a deep link** `/equipment/scan?item=<uuid>`: QR sticker encodes it, NFC = NDEF URL sticker carrying the SAME link (iPhone/Android open URL tags natively ⇒ zero NFC code; no Web NFC). D3 backup search by name / `serial_no` / `asset_tag` on the same screen. **D4 condition photos REQUIRED ≥1 both directions** (self-purchase precedent: optional evidence = 0/4 ever); คืน renders the out-photos for comparison. D5 units only (9 bulk rows excluded v1). D6 SA never edits registry `condition` — photos are the record. Grounding: `equipment_usage_logs` **0 rows ever** (spec 202 U2 never built; `check_out_equipment`/`check_in_equipment` live + verified), muster scanner field-proven 17/18 QR share. ⚠️ TWO fact-check blockers on record: `check_out_equipment` REFUSES unpriced items and `daily_rate` is **0/64** ⇒ no borrow door works until the 🔔 pricing call (spec header); and the `equipment-images` INSERT policy is back-office-only PLUS folder-depth=1 ⇒ `usage/<x>/` refused for everyone — U1 rewrites the predicate, not just the audience. U1 `equipment_usage_photos` + bucket-policy widen (⚠️ photos pin to the ORIGINAL log id across the check-in supersede — the reader must follow the chain) · U2 the scan route + both sheets (upload-then-RPC failure order; outcome states its result in place) · U3 QR label print page + NFC procedure in `docs/automations.md` · U4 entry points. Acceptance = fill-rate queries (scan share, photos-per-log, days-out), not a green suite. Detail in `370-equipment-scan-in-out.md`. |

| 371 | Review queue focus split — operator directive 2026-07-28: _"pm doesn't know where to focus on approval page, rejected items are counted as well"_. Measured live: `/review` shows, and the ภาพรวม hero counts, all **70** WPs at `pending_approval`, but only **52** are the PM's move (51 never-reviewed + 1 answered bounce); the other **18** are un-cured `needs_revision` bounces waiting on the site admin, and because the list orders by queue age **12 of them form one unbroken block at positions 2–13**, ahead of the first never-reviewed row — distinguished only by one small pill. The operator's "rejected" is those bounces: a true `rejected` decision leaves the queue outright (`decide_work_package` → `status='rework'`, spec 337 F3). Project is a degenerate axis (all 70 in one project) and trade, though populated, would still mix actionable with not-actionable inside each group — ⚠️ note `work_packages.category_id` FKs to `project_categories`, NOT `work_categories`, and joining the latter falsely reads as uncategorised. So the split is **whose move is it**: zone A `ตรวจได้ตอนนี้` (subgroups `พร้อมตรวจอีกครั้ง` then `รอตรวจครั้งแรก`, `รอมา N วัน` chips, one `เริ่มตรวจงานเก่าสุด` CTA) over a collapsed zone B `รอหน้างานถ่ายรูปใหม่` carrying the spec-355 reason chip + `ส่งกลับไป N วัน` and an explicit `ไม่นับในยอดรอตรวจ`. U1 = the page split (code-only). **U2 = the counts** — operator on seeing U1: _"Amount 70 items is misleading, how about separating them?"_ → new additive view `public.work_package_review_queue` (`security_invoker`, mig `075864`) is the ONE home of the zone predicate; the hero shows **52 ตรวจได้ตอนนี้ + รอหน้างาน 18** (both, attributed — not merely shrunk) and its label moves off `งานรออนุมัติ`, which is equally true of the rows now excluded; the badge head-counts `zone <> awaiting_site` (only its WP term — it sums two bank queues too). ⚠️ counts are RLS-scoped per viewer BY DESIGN: `can_see_project` is membership-gated only for `project_manager`/`site_admin`/`site_owner`/`auditor`, so a non-member PM sees 0 while the `project_directors` who work the queue see 70=52+18 — a pgTAP negative control must NOT use a `project_director`. Detail in `371-review-queue-focus.md`. |
| 372 | Approval decision model — operator directive 2026-07-28: _"upgrade the approval workflow to cover more scenarios"_ (① hide rework unless a rework rejection happened ② let the PM multiselect which phases/zones are wrong). Measured live: **163 `approved` · 54 `needs_revision` · 0 `rejected`, never once in five weeks** — so the whole rework subsystem (specs 216/248/337 F3, the หลังแก้ไข bucket, defect pairing) has NEVER fired: 0 of 397 WPs ever at `rework_round > 0`, 0 `defect` photos. `needs_revision` carries 100% of the load and is the crude instrument, so the leverage is making the USED path precise, not adding more rework machinery. The operator answered that `ส่งกลับแก้งาน` is unused because it is **unclear**, and the form supports that concretely: the three radios are not on one axis (`ถ่ายรูปใหม่` instructs the SA, `ส่งกลับแก้งาน` describes the PM's own action), the heavy option explains itself in round counters rather than consequences, and `premature` ("งานยังไม่เสร็จ", also 0 uses) already gives "the work isn't done" a home inside the OTHER button. Model = **one ไม่อนุมัติ door, four causes, three routes**: รูปไม่ครบ / รูปไม่ตรงงาน stay in the queue · **งานยังไม่เสร็จ → `in_progress`** · งานต้องแก้ไข → `rework` (the only door that turns rework on). ⚠️ Routing `premature` through `rework` was CONSIDERED AND REJECTED — the operator caught that it would open หลังแก้ไข uploads, making an SA file completion photos as after-**fix** for a repair that never happened; `in_progress` keeps `after` as the evidence and is already in the photo-deletable set. Targeting follows the CAUSE: phases for `incomplete` (absence — you cannot tap a photo that was never taken), photos for `mismatch` (median 10 photos on a bounced WP; the live comments say "ลบรูปที่ใช้อุปกรณ์อื่น", not the whole bucket). ⚠️ do NOT reuse `answers_photo_id` — it is bolted to `phase=defect`, which `addPhoto` refuses outside `rework`, dragging rework back through a side door. Zones are OUT of scope (no table exists; spec 366 is a draft). **U1 ✅ SHIPPED #824 → 0.256.4** (`photoSectionLabel`, round 0 ⇒ `รูปเพิ่มเติม`, five surfaces, 21 WPs / 151 legacy photos renamed not hidden). ▶ U2 form restructure (code-only) · U3 `premature`→`in_progress` (RPC body) · U4 cause-driven targeting (one child table). Detail in `372-approval-decision-model.md`. |
| 373 | /expenses finance scope — operator 2026-07-29: _"accounting team needs to see office expenses from users, to validate documents and inputted data to confirm and consolidate"_. Measured live: 19 expenses ฿23,776, 18/19 with receipt, **0/19 reviewed via spec 345, 19/19 reimbursements open** — and `/expenses`' list is `submitted_by=self` for EVERY role, so accounting saw nothing (RLS already admits `accounting`/`super_admin` firm-wide; the wall was UI-only ⇒ code-only at first ship; the §5 hard pay-gate later adds migs `075871`/`075872`). Design: finance-only `ของฉัน/ทั้งหมด` scope chips (pure resolver, exhaustive-domain test) · `listAllExpenses` + submitter + receipt chip + review-status chip joined from ONE `list_money_events_for_review(p_tab:'any', p_source_table:'office_expenses')` call on the AUTHED session (spec 345 stays SSOT, no write path here) · summary follows scope + payment-source subtotals over the enum with the EXISTING SSOT labels (บัตรเครดิต/สำรองจ่าย/บริษัทจ่ายตรง — ⚠️ payment source ⊥ reimbursement state: 16/19 open reimbursements are `company_card`) · month filter mirroring `/accounting/review` · **validate-before-pay**: reimburse-queue rows gain doc/review chips + voucher links, and (operator 2026-07-29) the HARD gate — `คืนเงินแล้ว` requires review = verified at button/action/RPC (migs `075871` gate + `075872` for-share lock) · voucher back chip goes referrer-aware (`?from=` + `safeBackHref`, registered in `MULTI_PARENT_DETAILS` — the route is already auto-discovered by `dynamicDetail`). ⚠️ fact-check catches folded: `submitterName` needs the admin-client seam (accounting's `users` RLS is self-read-only — spec-345 voucher precedent) · RPC `p_limit` clamps at 200, pass explicitly · RPC already returns `doc_count`, don't re-derive. Follow-ups ALL SHIPPED 2026-07-29: CSV export #839 · hard pay-gate. U1 scope+list+summary · U2 voucher door + queue chips. Detail in `373-expenses-finance-scope.md`. |

U1 (code-only): `/workers/[id]/attendance` month calendar gated `WORKER_ROSTER_ROLES` via the admin-client seam — (worker, date, session)-grain cells merged per day (in–out/method/OT/auto-out), header with `day_rate` + `entered_rate`-derived standard (money audience only), summary with ประมาณการค่าแรง and scanned-vs-recorded variance + the ยืนยันค่าแรง explainer (**`labor_logs` = 0 rows ever: `derive_muster_labor` shipped but skips all 31 unconfirmed workers**); roster door in U1, payroll door split to U1b (danger-path file, role-conditional — `/payroll` admits `accounting`, this page doesn't). | 375 | SA home — sort by movement, not by alphabet. Operator ask 2026-07-29 (_"redesign sa home"_ + _"why not have เบิกวัสดุ next to เบิกอุปกรณ์?"_); layout directions explored in Claude Design, shape **2b** chosen. Measured: `/sa` is the #2 route (**810 views/14d**, **511 `session_starts`**) and ends in **139 `งานของฉัน` rows sorted alphabetically** by `my-work.ts` (34 `in_progress` · 105 `not_started` · 1 `rework`), so **395 of ~810 visits leak sideways** to `/projects` → hub. ⚠️ **The app's ranking SSOT cannot fix it**: `deriveActionBand` maps `not_started` AND `in_progress` to one `todo` band, and **all 177 open leaf WPs are `priority='normal'`** so `rankFromPriority` is a constant — derived activity is the only signal with variance. Design: ⚠️ project block **built #846 then REVERTED #849** — its premise was FALSE (spec 313 U4 already redirects a `site_admin` from `/projects` straight to her hub, so the โครงการ tab is one tap; the "395 leak" misread an RSC redirect logging TWO `route_views` for ONE tap; and "4-item list" was dev-preview’s `super_admin` view — every real SA sees ONE project); ONE continuous list sorted by `greatest(last photo, updated_at)` with the sort **stated out loud**, a labelled rule at `ไม่มีรูปใน 14 วัน · N งาน` below which cold rows continue **dimmed but present** (demoted, never filtered); per-row 44px camera; `เบิกจากคลังหน้างาน` custody pair (`เบิกวัสดุ` | `เบิกอุปกรณ์`) **renaming** the `คลัง` tile — one door only, spec-313 U3 lesson; `ReportIssueFab` (0 `site_issues`/30d) → tile. U1 sort+rule · U2 project block · U3 custody pair (after #843) · U4 FAB→tile · U5 per-row camera. **CODE-ONLY, no schema, no new route.** ⚠️ Three mockup ideas REFUTED pre-build: `เกินกำหนด` red chip is dead (**175 of 177 have `planned_end` and all 175 are overdue** = 99% fire rate; spec 363 records the operator's _no dates on the SA screen_ ruling) · `4/6 รูป` has **no denominator in the codebase** · location-first tiles need **spec 366 zones** (the group tree is trade-based: งานฐานราก 49 · งานบ่อพักน้ำฝน 22). Refuted hypothesis recorded in §1.4: the empty sections (`แผนวันนี้`/muster/`ปัญหา`) already `return null` and cost nothing. Detail in `375-sa-home-movement-sort.md`. |

| 376 | On-site nav redesign — operator 2026-07-30 _"redesign nav system for on site users"_, scope operator-picked: **`site_admin` · technician · DC workers · storekeeper · site owner**. Measured 14d: SA traffic is ~4,000 of ~5,700 views inside the project world (WP detail 2,386 · hub 930 · `/projects` 448 — mostly the U4-redirect double-log echo, the refuted-#846 artifact) while **`/technician` = 13 views across 6 active users** — the biggest login cohort has a dead one-scroll portal and no tab bar at all. D1: **storekeeper is an SA hat, not a role** (17-value enum has none; คลัง = SA custody) — surfaced as a คลังหน้างาน cluster on the project hub (รับของ 153 views IS the storekeeper's real work), explicitly NOT a 6th tab. D2: **`site_owner` = site-oversight home** — roleHome resolver → her project hub (`can_see_project` membership arm verified live) + a coordinator-shaped 2-tab set; **revises parked 313 U6's `site_owner` half (was `/expenses`); the auditor half stays parked untouched**. D3: technician gets a real 3-tab bar — หน้าหลัก · **ประวัติ** · โปรไฟล์ (operator rejected เงิน — no money word on a worker's screen; `/technician/history` splits the wage/receipts/bank half out). D5: the no-login ช่าง cohort (SAME people as technician, pre-login — 17 of 31 `workers` rows unbound) navigates by printed QR only → canonical entry map (§6) + the shared-phone register interstitial (the known 328-pilot risk: a register QR on a phone carrying someone else's live session); storekeeper may become a real role later, so U2 gates via existing named role sets, never inline literals. U1 SA โครงการ tab direct-resolve (kills the tab-path redirect hop; `?view=all` hub + redirect stay for other doors) · U2 hub คลังหน้างาน cluster · U3 technician tabs + route split · U4 shared-phone interstitial + QR map · U5 `site_owner` landing + page-gate audit (**danger: role-home.ts**). CODE-ONLY, no schema. Detail in `376-onsite-nav-redesign.md`. |

| 377 | WP Brief v1 (ข้อมูลงาน) — structured definition-of-done per งานย่อย: scope (รวม/ไม่รวม) · quantity+location citing sheet+rev · binary acceptance criteria · evidence plan (required photo slots mapped to criteria) · typed reference attachments (ADR 0086 registry: `sheet_crop` · **EXPERIMENTAL** `iso_render`/`model_3d` · `bar_schedule` · other; files are the engine port, no 3D/AI API ever; GLB download-only, ISO PNGs are the field format). Published versions IMMUTABLE (append-only, attributed — the version row IS the publish event; `approvals` disqualified, ADR 0086 §4). SA reads it at the top of the รูปถ่าย tab (NO new tab — 363's 3-tab set stands), criteria collapsed, submit gate SOFT ("ยังขาดรูป N จุด" warns, never blocks — RULED 2026-07-30); PM review shows the same criteria with slot photo counts (355 tie-in; per-photo↔criterion binding OUT). Authoring = clone-from-previous-project → PD deltas → PD publishes; `work_category` fallback via the `project_categories.work_category_id` chain (⚠️ never join `work_categories` directly — the 371 trap); AI seeds only. Stale chip when a cited sheet rev is superseded (register = U4). 3D kill condition locked: steel-detail WPs of the next TFM branch, assets published BEFORE start, window ≤4 weeks, pass = ≥10 field-role opens (≈ `site_admin` — crews have no logins, ADR 0033) or foreman feedback; fail → dormant, nothing to rip out. U1 schema 🔔 · U2 authoring · U3 SA+PM surfaces · U4 register+uploads+dial+usage signal · U5 optional bar-schedule rows. Detail in `377-wp-brief.md`. |
| 380 | Missing purchase-doc visibility (ตามเอกสารซื้อ) — per-supplier-class accounting-doc requirements, RD-grounded (VAT → ใบกำกับภาษีเต็มรูป ม.86/4; non-VAT → บิลเงินสด ม.105 ทวิ / ใบสำคัญรับเงิน+สำเนาบัตร / ใบรับรองแทนใบเสร็จ; fallback ladder when the supplier refuses; `delivery_note`/สลิป never satisfy — ม.65 ตรี (18)). Detail in `380-purchase-doc-chase.md`. |
| 378 | Worker identity photo, captured by the site admin — operator 2026-07-30, the missing prerequisite of _"displaying profile image of the technicians scanned"_. Gate-checked live: **there is no picture of a worker anywhere** — `workers` has no photo/image/avatar column (all 27 enumerated), no worker-photo bucket exists (13 buckets), and the only image is `users.line_avatar_url`, which needs a login: **14 of 31 active workers bound, 12 with an avatar (~39%)** — a self-chosen LINE picture is the wrong artifact for an identity check and is missing for exactly the 17 phoneless workers the muster flow serves. ⚠️ **The named failure to avoid is spec 248** (`answers_photo_id` holds **0 of 2,712** because it needed a curatorial act): capture must ride a flow the SA is already in, so D5 puts it in the add-a-worker sheet (beside the passbook shot that RPC already demands) and on muster rows, and builds **no** bulk-photograph screen. ⚠️ **D3 is the finding that prevents an affordance-then-refuse bug: `create_worker`/`update_worker` gate on `is_back_office`, whose live membership EXCLUDES `site_admin`** — the one person holding the phone. Hence a new `set_worker_photo` RPC gated `(site_admin, super_admin, procurement_manager)` + `can_see_project`, the verbatim gate on `sa_add_project_worker_with_bank`. Storage reuses `contact-docs` under a `worker-photo/` prefix (the existing `sa-bank-capture/` pattern, 14 objects), and **D4 delegates the policy to a new `is_sa_capture_role` helper instead of restating the role array — repointing the bank-capture policy too**, since an inlined array in a `storage.objects` policy is what caused #823. Schema: one column + a one-column PII-wall widen (the spec-357-U-F `gender` precedent) + helper + policy + RPC. 🔔 **PDPA (§5) is BLOCKING** — consent/notice, retention and deletion-on-request are unanswered, and collecting personal data first is the wrong order. Acceptance is a fill-rate query (**baseline 0 of 31**), not a green suite. Detail in `378-worker-identity-photo.md`. |
| 379 | Undo a muster check-in — operator 2026-07-30, the _"confirm or edit"_ half (the "add on each scan" half already works: one `musterScan` per decode). Gate-checked live: of the **seven** muster RPCs **none offers a per-worker retraction** — the only repairs are _move_ (asserts a different team) or _check out_ (asserts they left); neither says "this did not happen". (⚠️ one non-RPC path does delete attendance: `muster_attendance_team_id_fkey` is `ON DELETE CASCADE` on `muster_teams` — silently and with no audit trace.) The SA is already forcing corrections through the wrong door: **`move_muster_worker` used 13× since 2026-07-19**. ⚠️ More likely now that #860 made the scanner continuous — she sweeps a line without watching the screen. Money framing: attendance feeds `derive_muster_labor` → `labor_logs` → wages, inert today only because 0 of 31 workers are cost-confirmed, so **building the undo before spec 369 U2 opens that gate is far cheaper than repairing money after**. Model: one DEFINER RPC `muster_undo_scan(worker, date, session)` (the live unique key is `(worker_id, work_date, session)`) that **deletes** the row after writing the whole row into `audit_log` (`crew_change` + `kind:muster_undo`) — `muster_attendance` has **no triggers** and is not append-only, and a tombstone column would silently mislead every reader that forgot the filter. Guards: role parity with `muster_scan_in` + `can_see_project` · refuse once the day is CLOSED (⚠️ `labor_logs.source_muster_id` has **no FK** — verified zero constraints — so nothing would cascade) · refuse if a `labor_logs` row points at it · refuse undoing a `regular` session while an `ot` row exists (OT is defined as continuing it). Two two-tap doors: the sweep tally row and the member row; ⚠️ the undo must also retract from `addedIds`/`addedRef` and carry the sweep generation, or the tally and the close-refresh count go wrong. Acceptance = undos rise while moves fall (**baseline 0 undos / 13 moves**). Detail in `379-muster-undo-check-in.md`. |

| 381 | Per-item equipment log history (ประวัติอุปกรณ์) — the fourth operator follow-up to the 367 reset, and time-critical: the registry was re-created by hand on 2026-07-30, so a trail started now is complete from birth. Two live findings shape it: `audit_log` SELECT admits only `super_admin`/PD/accounting/PM (plus a 2-event wp-rework arm), so **procurement, `procurement_manager` and `site_admin` cannot read a single equipment audit row** ⇒ read through a SECURITY DEFINER `equipment_item_history(p_item_id)` RPC, not a direct select; and `authenticated` holds SELECT-only on `audit_log` (no INSERT grant, no INSERT policy) ⇒ the audit **trigger must be SECURITY DEFINER** or every item edit 42501s. Plain edits (name/category/owner/status/tracking/`asset_tag`/image) are unaudited UPDATEs today and leave NO trace — that is the hole. Money gated INSIDE the RPC (rate events omitted, not redacted, for non-back-office — ADR 0055 d6). U1 trigger+RPC+pgTAP (schema) · U2 ประวัติ sheet off the row cluster · U3 condition photos (deferred). Detail in `381-equipment-item-history.md`. |

| 382 | Four photos per equipment item (ชุดรูปอุปกรณ์) — operator, 2026-07-30: รูปอุปกรณ์ · เพลทเลขเครื่อง (serial legible) · ยี่ห้อ · **QR ของเรา photographed ON the machine** (proof the spec-370 sticker was applied + the link between label and row), with DRAWN sample guides.
