Decisions: ONE photo per kind, replaceable (`unique(item_id,kind)` — keeps "documented?" countable) · OPTIONAL, never blocking a save, completeness as a `รูป n/4` chip which doubles as the PRI-transfer readiness query · drawn guides not reference photos (one real machine misleads for every other type). `equipment_items.image_path` is kept as a trigger-maintained MIRROR of the `item` kind so the CSV export's hasImage, the page thumbnail and the 367 pins keep working — and photo swaps land in the 381 history for free. Paths unchanged (`<itemId>/<file>`, depth 1) so adding kinds is not a storage-policy change. U1 schema+mirror+pgTAP · U2 four-slot control + chip · U3 bulk gap view. Detail in `382-equipment-photo-set.md`. |

| 383 | Equipment documents & expiries (เอกสารและวันหมดอายุของอุปกรณ์) — operator, 2026-07-30: _"each equipment may have warrantees, how do we record that? explore other information we might have missed."_ Live gate-check: `equipment_items` has **no warranty field, no expiry of any kind and no document attachment** (all 21 columns enumerated), and the four `equipment_item_photos` kinds are a closed completeness counter, not a document home. ⚠️ **`equipment-images` accepts `image/*` ONLY**, so a supplier's PDF warranty cannot be stored today — that is the one real fork (D4: a new `equipment-docs` bucket, recommended, vs photographing the paper). ★ **The finding that reshaped the spec: this model already exists** — `company_document_types` (35 curated types, each with `requires_expiry`) + `company_documents` (`type_id · issued_at · expires_at · storage_path · superseded_by`) + spec 331's expiry-aware `missingRequiredTypes`, which already encodes that an EXPIRED document must not satisfy a requirement. So the first draft's bespoke `equipment_item_terms` enum was withdrawn in favour of mirroring that model at ITEM scope: a curated registry (the operator discovers types — crane cert, พ.ร.บ., calibration — and an enum costs a migration each time) with renewals APPENDING via `superseded_by`. ⭐ **Timing is the argument: `equipment_items` is at 0 rows and the operator is about to re-type the fleet by hand, so a field settled now costs one line on the typing sheet and a field added later costs a second walk around the yard** (the spec-382 precedent). Seeds 8 types (warranty · inspection ปจ. · insurance · registration/พ.ร.บ. · calibration · purchase doc · manual · service record) — ✅ **all four open questions DECIDED 2026-07-30, U1 unblocked** (§8): six types seeded — `MANUAL`+`SERVICE_RECORD` dropped because each survivor must carry an expiry or a named consumer, the spec-248 `answers_photo_id` 0-of-2712 lesson · new `equipment-docs` bucket accepting PDFs · `registration_no` IN (the plate is readable exactly once, while someone stands in front of the machine) · **procurement chases expiries, forced by the live enum's 17 values containing no safety role** · U3 ships in the SAME release as U1+U2, since a document store with no deadline is a filing cabinet. NOT column-granted (the 382 trap: copying `equipment_items`' pattern would make the whole table invisible to PostgREST). Paths stay depth 1 or the storage policy 403s silently. Also swept but deliberately excluded, each with a reason: `registration_no` (needs "do you own registered vehicles?") · hour meter (no usage rows ever) · responsible-person-at-rest · and ⚠️ the `acquisition_cost`/`acquired_at` write path, which is NOT new and BLOCKS the PRI transfer pricing (spec 367 §10.4). U1 schema+bucket+seeds · U2 เอกสาร sheet off the row cluster · U3 expiry chip + list (the unit that makes the data worth entering) · U4 type-registry editor. Acceptance is a FILL RATE. Detail in `383-equipment-item-documents.md`. |

| 384 | ต้องแก้ไข arrives in batches, not one at a time — operator, 2026-07-31: _"sa home is now an infinite scroll"_, refined to _"main problem is with the rejected WPs that require attention"_. Measured live: the SA home is **~27,000px ≈ 40 phone screens**, and the top **~6,000px** is `SaActionSection` rendering **38 cards** (36 `needs_revision` + 2 `rework`; **zero `rejected` — that arm has never fired on live data**) above the muster strip, the plan, the custody pair and the tools grid. The section's own comment states the assumption it was built on — _"pinned above งานของฉัน, each row self-explaining"_ — which is right for 1–3 items and wrong for 16: the PM reviews in **sweeps** (07-21: 16 · 07-27: 12 · 07-28: 13), so the SA receives a dozen-plus bounces at once, most carrying the SAME reason and the SAME CTA, rendered as a dozen-plus identical self-explaining cards. Reasons today: รูปไม่ครบ 16 · รูปไม่ตรงกับงาน 13 · reasonless 7 · งานแก้ไข 2. ⭐ **The find that shaped the design: the age distribution is bimodal with an EMPTY interval** — a sweep clears within its own cycle or not at all — so the cut goes in that gap and §6 re-runs the query as acceptance rather than freezing the constant. 🚨 **And the UNIT of that derivation is part of the derivation, which this spec got wrong first:** the histogram was read as `now()::date - decided_at::date` ("29 rows at 1–3 days, 7 at 6–10, nothing at 4–5") and the constant set to 3 — but calendar-date subtraction returns 3 for anything from 3.0 to **3.99 elapsed** days, and the code compares elapsed instants, so the cut sliced the fresh sweep by time of day and the band rendered **18 of 36 rows instead of 7**. Re-measured as `now() - decided_at` (18/7/7/7 above 3/4/5/6 days) the empty interval is `[4, 6)`, so the constant is **5**, dead centre. ⚠️ **The whole vitest suite was green at 3 and still is at 5** — synthetic fixtures encode whatever unit their author had in mind; **rendering the real page is what caught it.** ⚠️ **The reasonless-rows-go-stale reading is a total confound and is called out as one**: spec 355 shipped the reason on 07-24, so reasonless ⇔ older by construction. Design = stale band always expanded (full cards, oldest first) + fresh batches collapsed to one row per reason (≤3 members renders expanded — hiding two cards costs more than it saves), ~6,000px → ~700px with no row removed; on `/sa` and NOT a new route, because the measured failure mode is the opposite one (spec 339 U1's correct detector sat on a page opened 70 times against 810 `/sa` visits, and `/sa/registrations` takes 7 of 855). ⭐ **Out-of-scope find, U2: the camera FAB cannot open a single one of the 38** — `captureWps` is built from `rest`, which is `inPlay` (already minus `pending_approval`) minus `rework`, i.e. **exactly the complement of `actions`**, so the SA's most-used control declines to offer the 38 WPs that were explicitly asked for photos. Mirrors [371](371-review-queue-focus.md) on the PM side (zones + `REVIEW_START_HERE_CTA`, one vocabulary for the two ends of one loop); `งานของฉัน`'s other 21,000px stays with [375](375-sa-home-movement-sort.md). CODE-ONLY: no schema, no route, no RPC — U1 adds ONE column to one existing `select` (the reopen audit row's `created_at`; `decided_at` is already fetched). Acceptance is a FILL RATE (baseline **14** lifetime `wp_evidence_resubmitted`, **36 open / 24 closed** of 60 ever-bounced) — flat after ship means the wall was not what stopped her and the next unit is batch resubmit, not more layout. Detail in `384-sa-fix-queue-batches.md`. |
| 385 | ทะเบียนเครื่องมือ/เครื่องจักร as a SKU catalog — operator, 2026-07-31, correcting the same day's instance-grain seed: _"เครื่องตบดิน is supposed to be only 1 in ทะเบียน, as in SKU. then user can pick from ทะเบียน when they want to add new equipments"_ + _"do not seed the equipment items, only SKUs"_ + keyword `catalog` for family consistency (`catalog_items` · `equipment_catalog_items` · planned `rental_catalog_items`). NEW `equipment_catalog_items` = the SKU grain (name once · function-first category — the spec-367 §12 principle now binds the TYPE, units inherit · brand/model · `default_tracking` · money-walled `default_daily_rate`) with a partial unique index on `lower(trim(name)) where is_active` (the spec-344 dup disease, prevented at birth); `equipment_items` stays the instance grain (serial, condition, 382 photos, 370 QR, movements) and gains nullable `equipment_catalog_item_id`. Writes delegate to `is_back_office()`; reads add `site_admin`; the rate column has NO authenticated grant in either direction (ADR 0055 d6 mirror) so the U2 pick flow copies it server-side. **Seeded 39 SKUs** from the 2-branch tool sheet incl. มี-0 types (a SKU is what CAN be owned, not what is held); the 63 instance rows from earlier that day were deleted on the operator's direction (guarded data op, zero field data). U1 ✅ mig `20260813075887` + pgTAP (spec+U1 one PR) · U2 pick-from-ทะเบียน add flow · U3 catalog manage surface · U4 tighten FK NOT NULL. Detail in `385-equipment-catalog.md`. |
| 386 | Telegram self-serve binding (เชื่อม Telegram ด้วยตัวเอง) — reverses [spec 318](318-notification-onboarding-settings.md) §5, which deferred this and left `telegram_chat_id` operator-set. ⚠️ **The gate-check reshaped the spec before a line was written: the Telegram channel has NEVER executed in production.** `notification_outbox` holds **zero rows all-time** with a `Telegram …` `last_error`, while **68 `wp_pending_approval` rows FAILED since 2026-07-22** on LINE errors — and the one `telegram_chat_id` holder is `super_admin` ⇒ in `orgWidePmIds` ⇒ a recipient of every one of them, muted for `pr_created` only. Either outcome (success ⇒ `sent`, failure ⇒ a `Telegram` error) would have left a trace; neither exists ⇒ `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` is unset in Vercel prod, the 2026-06-26 activation step nobody did. ⭐ **`status='sent'` was deliberately not used as evidence** — `rowOutcomeAfterPushes` returns `sent` at `recipientCount === 0` (`drain-policy.ts:32`), which is exactly what the 24 `wp_decision` "sent" rows in the outage window are. ⭐ **The finding that set the audience: LINE cannot reach the people who decide.** All 14 OA friends org-wide are 11 technicians + 1 accounting + 1 `site_admin` + 1 visitor — **zero** among `super_admin`, `project_director`, `project_manager`, `procurement_manager`, procurement, legal (office tier: 1 friend / 6 not / 9 unprobed of 16). So for the office tier Telegram is not a fallback behind LINE, it is the only channel with a path to working, while a Telegram nag aimed at technicians would be noise. Operator decisions 2026-07-31: a **new dedicated bot** (the token in `.telegram.env` is CC's own progress bot, `getMe` → `VAP CC Bot` / @`VAPTest_bot` — and `setWebhook` is exclusive per bot, so reusing it would route the operator's own CC messages into the app) · nudge audience = **office users**. Binding needs schema because `authenticated` holds **no UPDATE grant on `users` at all**, so `telegram_chat_id` is unwritable by every app path — bind AND unbind are DEFINER RPCs. Bot API constraints verified against the docs, not memory: `start` payload ≤ **64 chars** of `[A-Za-z0-9*-]`(base64url of 32 bytes = 43, enforced by a CHECK) and`setWebhook(secret_token)`→ the`X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token`header, which is the webhook's **only** authentication — nothing in the update body is trustworthy. U0 operator (BotFather +`TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`in Vercel) · U1 ✅ mig`20260813075888`token store + 3 RPCs + pgTAP · U2 webhook (danger path) · U3 settings bind/unbind/test · U4 role-scoped nudge on the existing readiness banner. **Acceptance is a FILL RATE:**`count(telegram_chat_id)`must move off **1 of 40**; delivery is a separate acceptance and needs U0. Detail in`386-telegram-self-serve-binding.md`. |
| 387 | Notification delivery health (ระบบส่งการแจ้งเตือนมีปัญหา) — the follow-up [386](386-telegram-self-serve-binding.md) earned: LINE push died org-wide 2026-07-22, **404 outbox rows failed, and nobody knew for ten days** because nothing in the product surfaces the pipeline's health — and the channel that would normally carry such an alert is the one that breaks, so the signal must be a READ the operator walks up to. ⭐ **The metric IS the design, and the obvious one is a trap:** "last successful delivery" cannot be answered by this outbox, because `rowOutcomeAfterPushes`stamps a row`sent`at`recipientCount === 0` (`drain-policy.ts:32`). Measured live on day ten of a total blackout, `max(sent_at) where status='sent' and last_error is null`returned **2026-07-30 — one day old** — while the last genuine LINE delivery was 07-21 22:58; a last-success tile would have shown GREEN throughout. So it keys on the FAILURE side, which is unambiguous. ⭐ **Threshold from the live distribution, not taste:** the three weeks before the break ran **0 failures across 1,400 terminal rows** (330/0 · 614/0 · 456/0), then 584/256 and 167/141 — so`degraded = failed > 0`is not a 99%-fire-rate badge (the spec 375`เกินกำหนด`defect) and fires on day ONE where a rate threshold would have waited. ⭐ **Placement was decided by telemetry, against the obvious home:** spec 283's`/settings/integrity`has **0 `route_views` in 21 days** (`super_admin`-only, audience of two) while the SAME `super_admin` opens`/settings`**231 times** — a correct detector on a page nobody stands on is not a shipped feature (spec 339 U1), so the notice lands on`/settings`inside the existing การแจ้งเตือน group. Silent unless there are failures, renders NOTHING when its own probe fails, names the verbatim`last_error`(a quota and a bad token need opposite responses), and says plainly that retrying will not help. U1 = the sibling honest-copy fix:`sendTestNotification`mapped a **429 monthly-quota** refusal to`กรุณาลองใหม่`, telling users to re-press a button guaranteed to refuse for the rest of the month. No schema — `authenticated`holds no grant on`notification_outbox`, so the read uses the service-role seam. ⚠️ `src/lib/notifications/**` is a danger path; the file sits where it belongs semantically rather than being moved to dodge the guard. **Acceptance is visibility, not a suite** — verified live: the notice renders `218 จาก 244 รายการใน 7 วันล่าสุด` with the LINE quota text, and must vanish by itself when the channel recovers. §6 flags the real underlying gap for its own spec: the outbox cannot distinguish "delivered" from "had nobody to deliver to". Detail in `387-notification-delivery-health.md`. |

| 388 | ช่าง nav: two tabs, and ประวัติ becomes attendance — operator, 2026-08-01: _"redesign technician's nav system"_, scoped in chat to the bar itself, then _"for now, only check in/out ontime/delay"_ for the second destination. The sibling of [376](376-onsite-nav.md) U3, which gave the role its first three-tab bar two days earlier; this does not reverse that split, it corrects **what the second destination is** using the telemetry U3 did not have. ⭐ **The measurement is the spec: `/technician/history` has 0 route views ALL-TIME**, against 38 for `/technician`, and technicians logged **zero write events in 30 days** — while 29 workers were scanned at muster 189 times. Audited against live rows, two of that tab's three sections are empty _by construction_: `wage_payments` is **0 all-time** (the 306 money wall holds every worker until cost-confirm) and pending bank requests are 0 — its only live content is **รายการรอรับ, 11 receipts addressed to workers who have a login**, which is also **the only write a ช่าง owns anywhere in the app**, sitting behind a money-labelled tab nobody has ever tapped. `/profile` meanwhile is a drill-down serving as a tab (the app's only one) re-rendering the same QR the home already shows, and the home's own second card (`AssignedWorkCard`) is empty for **13 of 13** because `muster_team_wps` holds 5 links across 27 teams. Decisions: bar 3→2 (หน้าหลัก · **ตั้งค่า**), โปรไฟล์ demoted to the `/settings` leaf its own back chip always said it was — lighting a tab with no new match entry, since `SETTINGS_TAB.match` already contains `/profile` · **ช่าง gets a settings door**, closing the 🔔 left open by [#856](https://github.com/vap-ops/prc-ops/pull/856) (making an existing capability visible — `/settings` has no role gate) · ประวัติ = **attendance**, the one subject with real rows · receipts lifted to the page they actually open. ⚠️ **A ช่าง cannot read one row of their own attendance today** — `muster_attendance` has a single SELECT policy keyed on `can_see_project`, whose live body falls to `else false` for `technician` — so U1 is a self-scoped DEFINER RPC, schema lane. ⭐ **The gate-check found a live 2-day-old copy bug and it changed a decision:** `/settings/my-info` deliberately refuses to host a bound ช่าง's bank and instead points at **"หน้าหลักช่าง"** — but 376 U3 moved the bank OFF `/technician`, so that card has pointed at a bankless page since 07-30; the bank returns to the home rather than a third surface being invented. ⭐ **The late rule is derived, and its UNIT is stated**: no `start_time`/`shift` column exists anywhere in the schema, and the 131 regular check-ins in Bangkok wall clock run 07:38–07:59 (64) · 08:00–08:12 (61) · **empty 08:13–08:26** · 08:27–08:32 (4) · **empty** · 09:08 (2), so the cut sits dead centre of the empty interval at **08:15** (08:00 + 15 min grace) and marks 6 of 131 — where _08:00 sharp_ would have marked **66**, the 99%-fire-rate defect. Minutes-of-day in Asia/Bangkok, never a string compare against a timestamptz ([384](384-sa-fix-queue-batches.md)'s unit lesson, [375](375-sa-home-movement-sort.md)'s encoding trap). **A verdict renders only on QR rows** — 31 of 131 check-ins are `manual`, where the timestamp is the SA's tap, and the app must not tell a worker they were late on someone else's clock. Reuses [374](374-attendance-calendar.md)'s `attendance-month.ts` cell builder (spec-351 dual sessions, post-midnight OT out, Bangkok formatting) rather than growing a second one. U1 RPC+pgTAP · U2 attendance page + money re-homed · U3 bar/strip/home order. **Acceptance is a fill rate and a re-derivation**: the histogram is re-run at ship time, `/technician/history` views must leave 0, and acknowledged receipts must leave 0. Detail in `388-technician-nav-redesign.md`. |
| 389 | WP catalogue + reference stars (แคตตาล็อกงานมาตรฐาน + รูปตัวอย่าง) — operator, 2026-08-03: two new projects exist in prod with **zero WPs** (TFM นายาว + TFM กกกระทอน, both `active`) while โพธิ์ทอง holds 379 WPs and **2,526 photos over 241 WPs**; _"we gotta have to find a way to refer to the old WPs … like sample images"_ → _"PD can map new WP against previous project's WP … pick the images"_ → the multi-project correction that set the design: _"we will continue to make this sort of building multiple times … better images from various projects, not just one … giving stars to good images"_. ⭐ **The design move: the star hangs on a cross-project IDENTITY, not a project pair** — the Vol.5 code (455 = 54 main + 401 sub, row-by-row verified against the โพธิ์ทอง sheet this session) becomes **`wp_catalog_items`** (the `catalog_items`/`equipment_catalog_items` family, work-type SKU grain), every project's WP points at it via `work_packages.wp_catalog_item_id` (the `equipment_items` precedent), and `wp_catalog_reference_photos` stars at (photo, catalogue-item) grain — a photo starred anywhere surfaces on every project's WP of that work-type forever; project #5 needs zero mapping work. ⚠️ **The fact-check reshaped the spec:** `wp_templates` is a RETIRED name (break-glass mig `075857`, 2026-07-27) and is NOT reused · `boq_template` (spec 236, live, 0 rows) is the ESTIMATE grain — S10-U6 composes, doesn't compete · `work_packages.category_id` FKs **`project_categories`** (0 rows on both new projects ⇒ U3 creates them first) · the tree is buildable only via `import_wp_grouping`/service-role (`wp_hierarchy_guard`; `create_work_package` can't set `is_group`) · **`photo_logs` RLS blocks every cross-project read** (`can_see_project` = false outright for technician, membership-gated for SA) ⇒ the ตัวอย่างงาน read is a DEFINER RPC `get_wp_reference_photos` + server-side signed-URL mint, or D3 is false. Measured bridge: **328 of 455 codes auto-match live โพธิ์ทอง WPs by name (2,176 photos = 86% reachable)**, 25 drifted → PD hand-match, 102 new, 26 dropped → PD's map-or-leave. Starring = PD+`super_admin` DEFINER RPCs; viewing = everyone on the WP. `work_categories` tops out at W09 ⇒ U1 adds W10+W11+`code_prefix` (subsumes an uncommitted stray draft in the main repo tree). U1 schema · U2 seed (TSV lands in `data/`) · U3 instantiate the 2 projects · U4 PD mapping surface · U5 stars + ตัวอย่างงาน. **Acceptance is a fill rate**: 100% on the new projects, →~353/379 on โพธิ์ทอง, star count > 0 in week one. Detail in `389-wp-catalog.md`. |
| 390 | Per-channel notification switches (เลือกช่องทางการแจ้งเตือน) — operator, 2026-08-03: "I get noti on line and telegram at the same time, where can I set them up, I want to be able to switch them on/off". Not a bug: the drain fans out over ONE recipient list to BOTH channels unconditionally (`drain/route.ts:515-522`, then the two push loops at `:555-575`) — no dedupe, no preference, no primary channel; `sent` is `anySuccess`. [386](386-telegram-self-serve-binding.md) named this and scoped it out, correctly at the time — **one** user held both bindings and LINE was dead. Both facts changed: **4 users are now dual-bound (`super_admin` · `technician` · `project_director` · `procurement_manager`, against 36 LINE-only)** and LINE recovered at the quota roll (0 failed / 74 sent since 2026-08-01, newest failure 07-31 10:59Z `LINE 429 monthly limit`) — which is exactly why the duplication became visible now. ⛔ **Rejected: per-event × per-channel** — 12 catalog events ⇒ 24 switches on a phone, and the complaint has no per-event shape; the operator wants ONE channel. Chosen: a channel-level pair orthogonal to the existing mute, mirroring [318](318-notification-onboarding-settings.md) U3's contract exactly (absence of a row = ON, own-rows RLS, RPC-only writes) so day one changes nobody's behaviour. Not two booleans on `users` — `authenticated` holds **no UPDATE grant on `users` at all**, so that route needs a DEFINER RPC anyway and widens a table every session reads. 🚨 **The finding that reshaped the spec, caught by the fact-check against live rows: the floor must measure REACHABILITY, not boundness.** A "you may not switch off your last **bound** channel" floor reads as obviously right and is wrong — the `procurement_manager` among the four carries **`line_oa_friend = false`, verified**, and LINE 403s a non-friend, so that user could legally disable Telegram and land at zero real delivery _including for the locked safety event_ `site_issue_reported`. Org-wide the flag runs **15 true · 10 false · 15 null** ⇒ a quarter of the roster, not an edge case. LINE therefore counts only when `line_oa_friend is not false`, with **null = reachable** (it refreshes only at LINE login, so null means never probed — [386](386-telegram-self-serve-binding.md) U5's rule); the stale-`true` case is stated as a bounded limit, not papered over, and §2.3 was rewritten to stop resting on a guarantee the floor cannot make. ⭐ **Second hole, closed in the same unit: two individually-legal steps reach zero** — disable LINE (fine, Telegram is on), then unlink Telegram; the floor never fires because unlinking is a different RPC. `unlink_telegram()` is `create or replace`d to clear the caller's channel rows in that case (verified live first: one zero-arg overload, so replace is the right instrument). ⚠️ Asymmetry stated rather than hidden: **no path anywhere — app, RPC or trigger — clears `line_user_id`** (every write is NULL-only-set), so a LINE-only user's single switch is permanently on — the same guarantee the locked event already makes, one layer down. U1 schema + RPC + pgTAP (the reachability arm pinned with two fixtures differing in exactly one column, plus a positive control) · U2 drain filter + the switches in ONE PR (UI alone is a switch that lies; ⚠️ `src/lib/notifications/**` is a danger path ⇒ grant-merged). **Acceptance is a fill rate plus a zero-check**: preference rows must appear, duplicates must stop while LINE is healthy, and no user may end up with no reachable enabled channel — the one failure mode that is silent from the user's side. Detail in `390-notification-channel-switches.md`. |
| 391 | ตัวอย่างงาน fills itself (automatic reference photos) — operator, 2026-08-04: _"for the confirmed WP, can you default star to them? we don't want to add unnecessary workload to pd"_. Spec 389 U5 shipped the ⭐ affordance and the section; both work, and the section is empty anyway because filling it is a manual act nobody performed (`wp_catalog_reference_photos` = **0 rows**). `get_wp_reference_photos` gains a DERIVED fallback rather than a backfill (a backfill is a snapshot — next month's completions would re-open the hole): `after` + `after_fix` photos of `complete`, mapped, NON-GROUP WPs, newest first, capped at **4**. Explicit stars are ADDITIVE (they pin ahead, they never erase the rest); a PD may HIDE a photo, which gets **its own table** because `starred_by` is NOT NULL and the shipped `unstar_reference_photo` deletes by photo id with no guard, so a shared table would let un-star silently delete a hide. **Signature unchanged** ⇒ a true `CREATE OR REPLACE`, no overload, 389's grant assertions stay green. 🚨 **This spec was REWRITTEN after a refute-first fact-check killed two of its three "correctness" decisions** — §7 keeps the record: the rework-round filter is **inert today** (all **2,873** `photo_logs` rows are at round 0) and its cited evidence was wrong (those 10 `after_fix` WPs had ZERO rejections and are documented legacy free-capture); and the "world-readable" premise was **inverted** — `anon` cannot execute the reader at all and a green pgTAP already asserted it, so the proposed signed-in split would have been a permanent no-op. Real axis = which signed-in ROLES see cross-project photos; operator re-decided on corrected facts: 389 U5's wide choice **stands**, no gate. Live 2026-08-04: **144 of 455** catalog items covered ⇒ **432 of 1,075** mapped leaf WP pages gain examples (~60% still render nothing, by design); per item min 1 · median 3 · p90 7 · max 61, and the cap truncates **37 items / 220 photos**. **Acceptance is a fill rate** plus a zero-check: the hidden table staying near zero means the selection filter is good; a burst means the filter, not the affordance, needs work. Detail in `391-auto-reference-photos.md`. |
| 392 | Project zone maps (ผังโซนโครงการ) — operator, 2026-08-04: _"would it be great if we have an illustration feature for draftman to draw zones under each WP"_, then, over the first recommendation's objection: _"I still believe we must draw zones, you can help seed too. reason being, we repeat same project again and again, these zones just need minor adjustments in each project."_ ⭐ **The reuse argument is the spec, and it measures: 1,160 of 1,307 WPs (89%) sit under a name shared by 2+ of the 6 projects**, three of which are the same client and building type (TFM ×3) — so a zone map is drawn once per building type, not once per project, and `clone_wp_briefs_from_project` + `copy-work-packages-sheet.tsx` already establish cloning project structure as a habit here. **Zones already exist as prose: 446 WP names carry a location word** (`งานเข้าแบบพื้นลานด้านซ้ายของอาคาร`, `งานถอดแบบคอนกรีตห้องถังน้ำดี`) — unqueryable, untied to the drawing. ⛔ **Zone is an AXIS, not a parent**: a WP carries exactly one work-category (locked) and a zone spans concrete + steel + paint, so `project_zones` + `work_packages.zone_id` crosses the tree the way ADR 0080 crosses dept/role/level/position; `wp_briefs.location` (free text, 0 rows) was rejected for giving no rollup. 🚨 **The first recommendation argued AGAINST this feature on two true fill rates — `photo_markups` = 2 rows / 0 strokes ever against 2,873 `photo_logs`, and `wp_briefs` = 0 briefs / 0 attachments / 0 route views with the whole spec 377 authoring UI live — and was wrong**: both measure acts with NO reuse, so a zero fill rate is evidence about the act measured, not about its neighbourhood; **check the amortisation axis before letting a fill rate kill a feature.** Engine chosen by comparison, not default: **konva + react-konva (54 KB gz)** as a lazily-imported island (`ssr: false`) so it never enters the main bundle — `@excalidraw/excalidraw` (353 KB gz, 6.5×) rejected on size + a freehand-shaped element model, **tldraw rejected on LICENCE** (development-only by default; production needs a commercial licence, free tier keeps a watermark). ⭐ **The engine never sees the database** — geometry persists as `[0,1]` fractions, the convention `validate-markup.ts` already uses, so a background swap moves nothing and a clone lands on a differently-photographed site. Gates delegate to `is_manager(current_user_role())` + `can_see_project`, never a restated role array (the `catalog-images` #823 defect). ⚠️ A background upload makes `storage.objects` a fourth layer in another schema, and Thai zone names need the ASCII key sanitiser (#933). U1 schema · U2 editor island · U3 chip + rollup + filter · U4 clone + TFM seed + reviewable assignment (never a blind backfill of 1,160 rows). **Acceptance is a fill rate**: `zone_id` ≥ 60% on the newest project, non-zero views by a non-admin account, one map cloned by the operator. Detail in `392-project-zone-maps.md`. |
| 393 | Bar bending schedule (ใบดัดเหล็ก) — operator, 2026-08-04, handing over a photographed notebook page (a ⊓ profile, `35 / 45 / 35`, `18 ตัว`, `115 = 180 กก`): _"maybe also use it with drawing drafts if needed"_ → asked what the app must DO with it, chose **"be read + printed on site"** and, for weight, **"compute, but warn only"**. ⭐ **The numbers are the payload; the drawing is OUTPUT** — a photographed sketch keeps the picture and throws away the data, so the app stores rows (`wp_bar_schedule_lines`: shape code, leg lengths, qty, optional `catalog_item_id`) and RENDERS the shape from them, which is what makes it correctable, summable and printable per zone. ⛔ Not a canvas: [392](392-project-zone-maps.md) earns one because its artifact is a layout that repeats across projects; a bending schedule is a table. Purchasing rollup and cut/bent/delivered workflow both offered and both declined — this spec touches no money path. ⛔ **Not on `wp_briefs`**: brief versions are immutable published snapshots and a bending schedule is revised after publication (the seeded `bar_schedule` attachment type stays the home for the SCANNED paper, not the structured rows); not project-level either, per the WP-centric principle. The weight rule worked against the operator's own sheet at DB32: line 2 (`20/120/20 ×10`, 16.0 m) computes **101 kg** against 100 กก typed — agrees, 1% — while line 1 (`35/45/35 ×18`, 20.7 m) computes **131 kg** against 180 กก typed, an 8.7 kg/m figure matching no standard rebar size ⇒ **flagged at 38%, blocked never**. ⭐ Mass per metre comes from `catalog_items.spec_attrs`, not a `d²/162` constant in the code, and **an absent catalog link produces no flag at all — which must never render as agreement.** ⚠️ The shape `switch` is exhaustive with no `default:` arm (a `default: return null` ships an invisible shape with a green typecheck). U1 schema · U2 pure renderer + calculator (unit-tested against both sketch lines) · U3 WP-detail section + editor · U4 print view. Detail in `393-bar-bending-schedule.md`. |
| 394 | เลือกรูปเข้ารายงานลูกค้า (per-photo selection for the client report) — 🏁 **COMPLETE: U0 #944 (the PDF leads with the Thai work name) · U1 #947 (mig `20260813075904`: `report_selected_photos` + RLS + 3 DEFINER RPCs, pgTAP 47/47) · U2+U3 #950 (toggle + arrange strip + 4th mode + cover note).** ⭐ Carry: a pre-insert guard and the job consuming its answer must agree on the same SCOPE — the zero-selected guard counted project-wide while the job narrows to `complete` WPs, which would have delivered a header-only PDF; a `selected` job resolving zero photos now fails instead. ⚠️ The frozen Railway worker ignores `reports.params`, so a fallback-built `selected` report returns every after-photo of every complete WP. 🔔 Open: `defect` photos are selectable (§5 names before/during/after only). ⛔ The PROJECT header stays code-first — operator ruling 2026-08-04, §11 "Answered"; do not re-raise. Operator: _"a way for pd to select 2 things, 1. starring for next project ref 2. selecting for client report generation"_. **Scoped to item 2** — item 1 is live in [391] (lane `refauto`), confirmed with the operator. ⭐ **The gate-check that reshaped it: `reports.params.photos` is a RULE, not a selection** (`"after" \| "all_phases" \| "none"`; `run-report-job.ts:77` expands it to a phase list and emits EVERY photo of those phases) — so per-photo report choice is genuinely absent, not a tweak. Two different judgments about one photo — _"good example of this KIND of work"_ (cross-project, permanent, the ⭐) vs _"show this to THIS client"_ (project-scoped, audience-facing) — so they get separate stores rather than one overloaded ⭐. Decisions: curated **per project** · **both toggles side by side on `/review`** in `PhaseGallery` (independent props: a PM sees no star, and an **unmapped** WP shows the report toggle only — 60 of โพธิ์ทอง's 379) · a **4th mode `เฉพาะที่เลือก`** leaving the existing three untouched. ⭐ **AMENDED the same day by the operator's omotenashi ruling — _"adding workload is ok, as long as it's omotenashi"_** — which REVERSES the original "no ordering" and adds D6–D8: **PD arranges photos within each WP** (sections stay in `code` order = spec 389 build order) · **a cover note per report** (lives in the existing `reports.params` jsonb ⇒ no schema change, and frozen with the document actually sent) · **the PDF leads with the Thai work name, demoting the internal code** (`build-pdf.ts:95` prints code-first today, so a client meets `S-07` before the work — costs NO PD effort, so it splits out as **U0** and ships without the schema lane). ⛔ Per-photo captions offered and **declined** — recorded so nobody "fixes" the omission. ⭐ The ruling also draws the line against 391's _"don't add unnecessary workload to pd"_: that surface is **internal** (audience = the next project's team) so auto-fill serves it, this one is **guest-facing**. ⭐ **Gate is `PM_ROLES` exactly** (operator: PM selects too) — that constant already IS `project_manager + super_admin + project_director`, so **no new `*_ROLES` export** and no spec-316 bijection trip; the asymmetry with starring is deliberate and verified live (star/hide/map all gate `('project_director','super_admin')`), so a PM curates a client's report but does not declare firm-wide exemplars. ⭐ **The store cannot be a flag on `photo_logs`** — `photo_logs_block_update` makes it genuinely append-only — and must not share the ⭐ table via a `purpose` column (different natural keys ⇒ a nullable CHECK-policed column, the mixed-content reference column CLAUDE.md bans). Own table mirroring `wp_catalog_hidden_reference_photos`; `project_id` deliberately derived, not stored. Zero-selected **refuses before inserting a report row** — no silent fallback to `after`, no empty PDF. ⚠️ **U2+U3 ship together** (a toggle that changes nothing, or a mode that can never be enabled, is an affordance promising something unbuilt); U1 is a migration and waits on the schema lane. Acceptance is a fill rate. Detail in `394-client-report-photo-selection.md`. |
| 395 | บัญชีรับเงินที่ไม่ใช่ของช่างเอง (non-own payout accounts) — operator, 2026-08-04: _"we need to flag the accounts that are abnormal though, we want to encourage them to have their own accounts"_, after the correcting rule _"some technicians use family's account temporarily"_ — so a non-matching holder is **NORMAL here and copy must never call it an error**; the ask is record-and-encourage (ADR 0061 financial inclusion), not forbid. Measured live 2026-08-04 (roster **grew 35→37→38→39 across the session** — dada was onboarding as it was scanned; **re-measure, do not inherit**), latest pass: 39 workers / 38 active / 37 with a bank number · 30 account names match normalised · **7 do not** · **0** have a bank number with a blank account name (the 2 seen earlier were filled in by a human mid-session — which is also why §8's "decide what to do about blanks" now has no instances). ⭐ **The concentration is the finding — and the honest number is 3, not 4: three workers share ONE account number** (`014162319729` — พิเชษฐ์/โนรี/สายฟ้า), while `อนันตชัย ทีฆายุทธสกุล`'s own row names the same holder on a DIFFERENT number (`044…`, almost certainly the same passbook mistyped). ⚠️ **The fact-check caught §1 and §3 disagreeing: the shared-account detector returns 3, so a headline of 4 would have shipped a number the primary detector cannot reproduce.** And **3 of those 4 rows carry the prefix `ด.ช.` (เด็กชาย, a minor)**. Possibly legitimate (a หัวหน้าชุด collecting for his crew is normal practice) but invisible today. ⚠️ **Name matching must NOT be the primary detector — 2 of the 8 are pure Thai spelling variance for the SAME person** (`สุรินทร์ นาคพันธุ์` vs `นางสุรินทร์ นาคพันธ์`; อนันตชัย's own row differs by one character), so a name flag would nag those two forever while saying nothing about concentration. Primary signal = **how many DISTINCT workers share one `bank_account_number`** — objective, spelling-immune, and the one with audit weight; name mismatch is advisory only. 🚨 **The discovery that shrank this spec: [320](320-payout-nominee.md) ALREADY BUILT the whole mechanism and it has never been used once.** `worker_payout_nominee` carries `payee_name`/`payee_relationship`/bank triple/`consent_doc_path`/`set_by`/`cleared_by`, four DEFINER RPCs exist, the table is zero-grant bank PII, and **`PAYOUT_NOMINEE_STALE_DAYS = 45` is already shipped as display-only reclaim pressure** — i.e. the "encourage them to get their own account" nudge was designed, built, and wired to no surface. **0 rows all-time.** ❌ **Not the gate** — all four RPCs admit `('procurement_manager','project_director','super_admin','procurement')`, verified live, so dada could have recorded a nominee today; ⚠️ `src/lib/payroll/payout-nominee.ts`'s comment claiming "procurement*manager-gated" is **STALE**, fix it in whichever unit touches the file. ✅ **Placement is the cause**: the bank fields sit on the `/workers` edit sheet where onboarding happens, the nominee control lives under `/settings/payout-nominees`, so the third-party account goes into `workers.bank*\*`— which silently asserts the account is the worker's own and bypasses the consent doc, the audit trail AND the 45-day nudge. Suspected secondary friction, **unconfirmed, ask the team first**:`consent*doc_path`is`NOT NULL`. Derived tri-state per worker (`own`·`nominee`·`unrecorded`), **no new column on `workers`**. U1 detector (pure, exhaustive over live shapes; ⚠️ pin the honorific list — `ด.ช.`appears in real data) · U2 badge · U3 the nominee control moves to the point of entry, reusing`set_worker_payout_nominee`· U4 work the existing mismatches by hand (spelling rows are CORRECTIONS, not nominees — the `044…`/`014…`near-miss is probably one). 🚨 **There is NO unit for the 45-day nudge: it ALREADY SHIPS.**`/settings/payout-nominees`imports`PAYOUT_NOMINEE_STALE_DAYS`and renders the highlighted age chip; an earlier draft of this spec proposed building that surface and the refute-first fact-check killed it — ⭐ **"dormant constant" is a claim about CONSUMERS, so grep for them before proposing to wire one up.** ⚠️ Also missed on the first pass:`/workers`is **not** the only door into`workers.bank*_`—`worker*bank_change_requests`+`decide_worker_bank_change` (spec 315) writes all three columns on a different gate (`is_manager or procurement_manager`), so U3 must close both or third-party accounts keep arriving. ⚠️ **U1→U2→U3 order is load-bearing — a badge whose control does not exist strands the reader with a flag and no way to resolve it.** ⚠️ **Gate: "call the RPCs' own gate, never restate it" was UNFOLLOWABLE as first written** — no constant with that membership exists (`STAFF_APPROVAL_ROLES`is the same set minus plain`procurement`; `BACK_OFFICE_ROLES`/`WORKER_ROSTER_ROLES`/`PAYROLL_ROLES`all add`project_manager`), and **both shipped nominee pages already pass the four literals inline to `requireRole`** ⇒ U1 creates `PAYOUT_NOMINEE_ROLES = [...STAFF_APPROVAL_ROLES, "procurement"]`and migrates them. ⚠️ The reader **cannot run in an RLS session at all**:`workers.bank*_` are zero-grant and a shared-account count is inherently cross-worker ⇒ admin/DEFINER seam, which §5's "a server reader" originally hid. Related but deliberately OUT of scope (§7): worker`นายเหิน เมืองงาม`has two records and record B is bound to user`aemon` (a different person) carrying aemon's account — and **there is no in-app way to unbind a worker from a user** (`create*worker`takes`p_user`, `update_worker`does not, no unbind RPC), so a mis-link is unfixable from the UI and will recur; its own spec. **Acceptance is a fill rate**: `unrecorded`→ 0 and nominee rows > 0 — today the ratio is 7-and-0. ⚠️ **Every count here is a SNAPSHOT that moved four times during one session** (roster 35→37→38→39; a human corrected the`สุรินทร์`row between two fact-check queries five minutes apart) — re-measure at build time, and state whether the detector filters`workers.active`(the`นายเหิน` row is inactive and would drop out silently). Detail in`395-non-own-payout-accounts.md`. |
| 396 | ปกป้องตัวตนของช่างที่ผูกบัญชีแล้ว (protecting a bound worker's identity) — 🚨 **a real employee's record was overwritten with another person's identity in prod, 2026-08-04.** aemon registered HERSELF on 08-02 (`เอมอร ฮามศรีพรม`); approving it correctly created her worker record (`PRC-26-0036`, bound to her own account, her own bank, audit `source: staff_registration`). On 08-04 procurement — hunting for a different worker — opened **that** row and set `name → นายเหิน เมืองงาม`, gender `male`, daily/temporary, then deactivated it. It still carries **aemon's phone and employee id**. ⭐ **Nothing was mis-bound: the binding was correct throughout. This was a mis-EDIT, not a mis-LINK** — an earlier reading called for an unbind capability and the audit log refuted it ([395](395-non-own-payout-accounts.md) §7 carries the correction; ⚠️ only `claim_worker_invite`writes`workers.user_id`, NOT the three functions a fact-check claimed). ⭐ **What held: the bank lock (`รออนุมัติจากคำขอของช่าง`) is the ONLY reason the wrong bank details did not follow the wrong name — the protection exists for money and stops at the identity.** That asymmetry is the spec. Measured blast radius: **15 of 39 workers are bound**; **22 back-office rename events all-time, 11 of them (50%) on a BOUND worker**, all by procurement on 08-04 — ⚠️ **and TEN of those eleven were legitimate** (a bulk `นาย`/`นาง` prefix normalisation, ten inside 90 seconds), the eleventh replaced a person twenty minutes later. **So the fix is NOT "block renames"** — the system simply cannot distinguish \_normalise this person's name* from _replace this person_, and neither can the operator, because nothing on screen says whose record it is. Second asymmetry: a worker changing their OWN name queues at `identity_change_requests` (**4 pending, undecided**) while back-office writes instantly. Two concrete holes: `workers/page.tsx:174` **destructures `user_id` out** and passes only a boolean `portalBound`, so the UI structurally cannot name the account holder (⚠️ surfacing it is a PDPA decision, §8 Q1); and the `worker_change` payload records only the **NEW** name — the original was recoverable here ONLY because an approved `staff_registrations` row happened to hold it. U1 audit the previous value (cheapest, makes the class RECOVERABLE not merely detectable) · U2 name the account holder · U3 confirm a non-normalising rename, ⚠️ **copy must not accuse** · U4 surface the 4 pending identity requests. ⚠️ U1 first — U2/U3 only reduce the rate. ❌ Non-goals: no unbind (`claim_worker_invite` + the `workers_user_id_key` unique partial index already guard it; `create_worker`'s `p_user` is unvalidated but **no app code passes it**), and do NOT route back-office renames through the approval queue. Detail in `396-bound-worker-identity-protection.md`. |

| 398 | รูปย่อที่เก็บเอง (self-hosted photo thumbnails) — the Supabase org badge read `EXCEEDING USAGE LIMITS`: **Storage Image Transformations 689 / 100 (689%)**, overage 589, every other metric fine. One cause — `mint-thumbnails.ts:39` mints a `transform:{320,320,contain}` signed URL per photo and Supabase counts DISTINCT ORIGIN IMAGES per cycle (2,768 objects, 2,268 uploaded in July). ✅ **Measured NOT broken**: the oldest object at an unusual 317×317 (uncacheable) still rendered `HTTP 200` 20 KB from a 1.2 MB original — the danger is that a future restriction goes SILENT, because the minter swallows the error and DROPS the photo. ⭐ **No migration needed: the `photos` bucket already allows `image/webp`, so thumbs live at a derived key in the SAME bucket** — code-only, no schema lane. U1 thumb-key SSOT + `sharp` generator + prod backfill (~1.7 GB one-time egress) · U2 generate-on-write via `after()` (stable since 15.1, Vercel `waitUntil`) so the ADR 0039 upload queue is NOT touched · U3 flip the reader and delete `transform:`. ⚠️ A miss must fall back to FULL-SIZE, never to the transform (leaves the meter armed) and never to nothing (both callers drop a photo with no URL). Detail in `398-self-hosted-photo-thumbnails.md`. |

unit): 25, 26, 29, 30. (235 = account-deactivation draft, parked separately.
267–269 rows land with their own PRs.)
